"""Project-wide constants and paths.

Single source of truth for the dataset contract documented in
``docs/brief-problema.md`` and the acceptance criteria of
``docs/brief-alineamiento-eda.md``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Paths
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DATA_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "data"
LABEL_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "label"
DOCS_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "docs"
RAW_CSV = DATA_DIR / "compras_data.csv"
DATA_DICTIONARY = LABEL_DIR / "Diccionario de Datos.txt"

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dataset contract
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

BRANDS: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5)

PRICE_COLS: list[str] = [f"precio_marca_{j}" for j in BRANDS]
PROMO_COLS: list[str] = [f"promo_marca_{j}" for j in BRANDS]

TARGET_INCIDENCE = "incidencia_compra"
TARGET_BRAND = "id_marca"
TARGET_QUANTITY = "cantidad"

KEY_CLIENT = "id"
KEY_TIME = "dia_visita"

DEMOGRAPHIC_COLS: list[str] = [
    "genero",
    "estado_civil",
    "edad",
    "nivel_educacion",
    "ingreso_anual",
    "ocupacion",
]

LAG_COLS: list[str] = ["ultima_marca_comprada", "ultima_cantidad_comprada"]

#: Excluded by explicit instruction of the data dictionary (control E1).
EXCLUDED_COLS: list[str] = ["tamanio_ciudad"]

#: Outcomes of the visit. Never features of the incidence model (controls E2, E3).
POST_OUTCOME_COLS: list[str] = [TARGET_BRAND, TARGET_QUANTITY]

#: Temporal split boundary (control E8). Train <= SPLIT_DAY, test > SPLIT_DAY.
SPLIT_DAY = 550

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/data.py  (271 lineas)

"""Data loading, schema validation and logical-consistency checks.

Covers controls E11 (logical consistency), D2 (static demographics) and E4/E5
(lag-column semantics) of ``docs/brief-alineamiento-eda.md``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as cfg


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Loading
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def load_raw(path=None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load the raw visit-level dataset, sorted by ``(id, dia_visita)``.

    Sorting is not cosmetic: every lag and expanding-window feature depends on
    this order, and so does the verification of ``ultima_marca_comprada``.
    """
    path = path or cfg.RAW_CSV
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    return df.sort_values([cfg.KEY_CLIENT, cfg.KEY_TIME]).reset_index(drop=True)


def load_data_dictionary(path=None) -> str:
    """Return the raw text of ``label/Diccionario de Datos.txt``."""
    path = path or cfg.DATA_DICTIONARY
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Schema profile
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def schema_profile(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One row per column: dtype, cardinality, range, nulls and assigned role.

    The ``rol`` column is what makes this table an alignment artifact rather
    than a generic ``df.info()``: it states what each column is *for* in the
    optimization problem.
    """
    roles = _column_roles()
    out = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "dtype": df.dtypes.astype(str),
            "n_unique": df.nunique(),
            "n_nulos": df.isna().sum(),
            "min": [df[c].min() if pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(df[c]) else None for c in df.columns],
            "max": [df[c].max() if pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(df[c]) else None for c in df.columns],
        }
    )
    out["rol"] = [roles.get(c, "feature") for c in out.index]
    return out


def _column_roles() -> dict[str, str]:
    roles: dict[str, str] = {
        cfg.KEY_CLIENT: "clave de cliente (agrupacion)",
        cfg.KEY_TIME: "eje temporal (split)",
        cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE: "TARGET M1 (pi)",
        cfg.TARGET_BRAND: "TARGET M2 (q) - post-resultado",
        cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY: "TARGET M3 (lambda) - post-resultado",
        "ultima_marca_comprada": "feature historial (lag verificado)",
        "ultima_cantidad_comprada": "feature historial (binaria, mal nombrada)",
    }
    for col in cfg.PRICE_COLS:
        roles[col] = "variable de decision y_jtk (precio)"
    for col in cfg.PROMO_COLS:
        roles[col] = "variable de decision z_jt (promocion)"
    for col in cfg.DEMOGRAPHIC_COLS:
        roles[col] = "feature / segmentacion M0"
    for col in cfg.EXCLUDED_COLS:
        roles[col] = "EXCLUIDA por diccionario (E1)"
    return roles


def dataset_overview(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
    """Headline facts of section 2.1 of the problem brief."""
    visits_per_client = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT).size()
    visits_per_day = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_TIME).size()
    return pd.Series(
        {
            "filas": len(df),
            "columnas": df.shape[1],
            "clientes_unicos": df[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique(),
            "dias_distintos": df[cfg.KEY_TIME].nunique(),
            "dia_min": int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()),
            "dia_max": int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()),
            "visitas_por_cliente_media": round(visits_per_client.mean(), 1),
            "visitas_por_cliente_min": int(visits_per_client.min()),
            "visitas_por_cliente_max": int(visits_per_client.max()),
            "visitas_por_dia_media": round(visits_per_day.mean(), 1),
            "visitas_por_dia_min": int(visits_per_day.min()),
            "visitas_por_dia_max": int(visits_per_day.max()),
            "nulos_totales": int(df.isna().sum().sum()),
            "duplicados_exactos": int(df.duplicated().sum()),
            "tasa_compra": round(df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean(), 4),
            "n_compras": int(df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].sum()),
        }
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Logical consistency -- control E11
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def consistency_checks(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Verify the implications that must hold between target and outcomes.

    A non-zero count in any row means the dataset does not behave as the data
    dictionary describes, and every downstream conditional universe is suspect.
    """
    bought = df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE] == 1
    not_bought = df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE] == 0

    checks = [
        ("compra=1 & cantidad=0", int((bought & (df[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY] == 0)).sum())),
        ("compra=1 & id_marca=0", int((bought & (df[cfg.TARGET_BRAND] == 0)).sum())),
        ("compra=0 & cantidad>0", int((not_bought & (df[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY] > 0)).sum())),
        ("compra=0 & id_marca>0", int((not_bought & (df[cfg.TARGET_BRAND] > 0)).sum())),
        ("id_marca fuera de 0-5", int((~df[cfg.TARGET_BRAND].isin([0, *cfg.BRANDS])).sum())),
        ("cantidad negativa", int((df[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY] < 0).sum())),
        ("precio <= 0", int((df[cfg.PRICE_COLS] <= 0).sum().sum())),
        ("promo fuera de 0/1", int((~df[cfg.PROMO_COLS].isin([0, 1])).sum().sum())),
    ]
    out = pd.DataFrame(checks, columns=["verificacion", "violaciones"])
    out["estado"] = out["violaciones"].map(lambda n: "OK" if n == 0 else "FALLA")
    return out


def static_demographics_check(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Control D2: demographics must be invariant within each client.

    If they are, client-level aggregation is legitimate and the demographic
    block can feed the segmentation model without collapsing information.
    """
    rows = []
    for col in cfg.DEMOGRAPHIC_COLS:
        n_varying = int((df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)[col].nunique() > 1).sum())
        rows.append({"variable": col, "clientes_con_mas_de_un_valor": n_varying,
                     "estado": "estatica" if n_varying == 0 else "VARIA"})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lag semantics -- controls E4 and E5
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def verify_lag_columns(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Control E4/E5: establish what the two ``ultima_*`` columns really are.

    ``ultima_marca_comprada`` is claimed to be the previously purchased brand.
    We test it against the true lag. ``ultima_cantidad_comprada`` is named as a
    quantity by the dictionary but is binary; we test whether it is in fact the
    lag of the purchase indicator.
    """
    d = df.sort_values([cfg.KEY_CLIENT, cfg.KEY_TIME])
    true_lag_brand = d.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)[cfg.TARGET_BRAND].shift(1).fillna(0)
    true_lag_incidence = d.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].shift(1).fillna(0)

    n_unique_qty = int(d["ultima_cantidad_comprada"].nunique())
    rows = [
        {
            "verificacion": "ultima_marca_comprada == shift(1) de id_marca por cliente",
            "coincidencia": round(float((d["ultima_marca_comprada"] == true_lag_brand).mean()), 4),
            "conclusion": "lag real, usable sin fuga" ,
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "ultima_marca_comprada == id_marca de la misma fila",
            "coincidencia": round(float((d["ultima_marca_comprada"] == d[cfg.TARGET_BRAND]).mean()), 4),
            "conclusion": "coincidencia parcial esperada (no es copia de la fila)",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": f"ultima_cantidad_comprada tiene {n_unique_qty} valores distintos",
            "coincidencia": float(n_unique_qty),
            "conclusion": "binaria: el diccionario la nombra mal" if n_unique_qty == 2 else "revisar",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "ultima_cantidad_comprada == shift(1) de incidencia_compra",
            "coincidencia": round(float((d["ultima_cantidad_comprada"] == true_lag_incidence).mean()), 4),
            "conclusion": "es el lag del indicador de compra, no una cantidad",
        },
    ]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def no_history_profile(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
    """Control E7: ``ultima_marca_comprada == 0`` is absence of history, not a null."""
    mask = df["ultima_marca_comprada"] == 0
    return pd.Series(
        {
            "filas_sin_historial": int(mask.sum()),
            "pct_sin_historial": round(100 * float(mask.mean()), 1),
            "de_ellas_primera_visita": int((mask & (df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT).cumcount() == 0)).sum()),
            "tasa_compra_sin_historial": round(float(df.loc[mask, cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()), 4),
            "tasa_compra_con_historial": round(float(df.loc[~mask, cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()), 4),
        }
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Derived helpers used across the EDA
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def add_paid_price_and_spend(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Attach ``precio_pagado`` and ``gasto`` to purchase rows (control A7).

    The paid price is the price of the brand actually chosen; it is undefined
    for non-purchases and left as ``NaN`` there on purpose.
    """
    out = df.copy()
    price_matrix = out[cfg.PRICE_COLS].to_numpy()
    brand_idx = out[cfg.TARGET_BRAND].to_numpy() - 1
    valid = brand_idx >= 0

    paid = pd.Series(np.nan, index=out.index, dtype="float64")
    paid.loc[valid] = price_matrix[valid, brand_idx[valid]]

    out["precio_pagado"] = paid
    out["gasto"] = out["precio_pagado"] * out[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY]
    return out


def purchases_only(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Conditional universe for models M2 and M3 (control E12)."""
    out = df[df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE] == 1].copy()
    if "precio_pagado" not in out.columns:
        out = add_paid_price_and_spend(out)
    return out


def temporal_split(df: pd.DataFrame, split_day: int | None = None) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Control E8: split by time, never at random.

    With ~117 visits per client, a random split puts visits of the same client
    on both sides and inflates every metric.
    """
    split_day = split_day or cfg.SPLIT_DAY
    train = df[df[cfg.KEY_TIME] <= split_day].copy()
    test = df[df[cfg.KEY_TIME] > split_day].copy()
    return train, test


def split_composition(df: pd.DataFrame, split_day: int | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Control E9: the split must not introduce composition bias."""
    split_day = split_day or cfg.SPLIT_DAY
    train, test = temporal_split(df, split_day)
    rows = []
    for name, part in (("train", train), ("test", test)):
        rows.append(
            {
                "split": name,
                "filas": len(part),
                "clientes": part[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique(),
                "dia_min": int(part[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()),
                "dia_max": int(part[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()),
                "tasa_compra": round(float(part[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()), 4),
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


==============================================================================
Fase 0 · Encuadre congelado

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/framing.py  (596 lineas)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""Phase 0 -- framing and preparation.

Freezes the four decisions that every later phase depends on, *before* any
model is fitted:

1. **Unit of analysis** -- ``cliente x visita``, verified unique on ``(id, dia_visita)``
2. **Target variable** -- ``incidencia_compra``, with the conditional universes of M2 and M3
3. **Admissible feature set** -- with a stated reason for every exclusion
4. **Validation strategy** -- temporal split frozen at day 550, plus GroupKFold by client

"Freezing" is literal: :func:`freeze` writes the contract and a fingerprint of
the split to ``artifacts/encuadre.json``, and :func:`verify_frozen` re-checks a
later dataset against it. A silent change in the data, the split boundary or
the feature set then surfaces as a failed verification instead of as an
unexplained jump in a metric.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from . import config as cfg

ARTIFACTS_DIR = cfg.PROJECT_ROOT / "artifacts"
FRAMING_PATH = ARTIFACTS_DIR / "encuadre.json"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The contract
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FramingContract:
    """The frozen Phase 0 decisions.

    Frozen as a dataclass so no later phase can mutate it in place: changing a
    decision means writing a new contract and saying so.
    """

    unidad_analisis: str = "cliente x visita"
    clave_unidad: tuple[str, ...] = (cfg.KEY_CLIENT, cfg.KEY_TIME)

    target_m1: str = cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE
    target_m2: str = cfg.TARGET_BRAND
    target_m3: str = cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY

    universo_m1: str = "todas las visitas (58,693)"
    universo_m2_m3: str = "visitas con incidencia_compra == 1 (14,638)"

    split_day: int = cfg.SPLIT_DAY
    split_tipo: str = "temporal (no aleatorio)"
    cv_estrategia: str = "TimeSeriesSplit en train + GroupKFold por id para clientes nuevos"

    metrica_principal: str = "PR-AUC"
    metricas_apoyo: tuple[str, ...] = ("ROC-AUC", "precision@k", "recall@k")
    metrica_prohibida: str = "accuracy (75.1% prediciendo siempre no-compra)"

    umbral_decision: str = "derivado del costo de contacto en P1, no fijado en 0.5"

    baseline_tasa_base: float = 0.2494
    baseline_targeting_lift: float = 2.43

    fecha_congelado: str = field(default_factory=lambda: date.today().isoformat())

    def as_table(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Render the contract as a two-column table for the notebook."""
        rows = []
        for key, value in asdict(self).items():
            if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
                value = ", ".join(str(v) for v in value)
            rows.append({"decision": key, "valor": value})
        return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Unit of analysis
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def verify_unit_of_analysis(df: pd.DataFrame, contract: FramingContract | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Verify that the unit of analysis is well defined.

    If ``(id, dia_visita)`` were not unique, every per-visit probability would
    be ill-posed and the lag features would silently mix rows. This check has
    to pass before anything else in Phase 0 means something.
    """
    contract = contract or FramingContract()
    keys = list(contract.clave_unidad)

    n_rows = len(df)
    n_groups = int(df.groupby(keys).ngroups)
    n_dup_rows = int(df.duplicated(subset=keys, keep=False).sum())

    rows = [
        {
            "verificacion": f"{' x '.join(keys)} identifica una fila unica",
            "observado": f"{n_groups:,} pares para {n_rows:,} filas",
            "estado": "OK" if n_groups == n_rows else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "filas en pares duplicados",
            "observado": f"{n_dup_rows:,}",
            "estado": "OK" if n_dup_rows == 0 else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "cobertura temporal por cliente",
            "observado": (
                f"{df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT).size().min()}-"
                f"{df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT).size().max()} visitas por cliente"
            ),
            "estado": "OK",
        },
    ]
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["unidad_valida"] = bool((out["estado"] == "OK").all())
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Target definition
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def target_definition(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The three targets, their universes and their class balance.

    Stating the universe per model is what prevents the most expensive mistake
    available here: training brand choice or quantity on rows where no purchase
    happened (control E12).
    """
    purchases = df[df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE] == 1]

    rows = [
        {
            "modelo": "M1 incidencia",
            "target": cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE,
            "tipo": "clasificacion binaria",
            "universo": "todas las visitas",
            "n_filas": len(df),
            "balance": f"{df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean():.4f} positivos",
            "parametro_que_alimenta": "pi_it",
        },
        {
            "modelo": "M2 eleccion de marca",
            "target": cfg.TARGET_BRAND,
            "tipo": "clasificacion multiclase (5)",
            "universo": "solo visitas con compra",
            "n_filas": len(purchases),
            "balance": " / ".join(
                f"{j}:{(purchases[cfg.TARGET_BRAND] == j).mean():.3f}" for j in cfg.BRANDS
            ),
            "parametro_que_alimenta": "q_ijt",
        },
        {
            "modelo": "M3 cantidad",
            "target": cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY,
            "tipo": "regresion / conteo",
            "universo": "solo visitas con compra",
            "n_filas": len(purchases),
            "balance": f"media {purchases[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY].mean():.2f}, max {int(purchases[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY].max())}",
            "parametro_que_alimenta": "lambda_ijt",
        },
        {
            "modelo": "M0 segmentacion",
            "target": "-- (no supervisado)",
            "tipo": "clustering",
            "universo": "500 clientes, demografia",
            "n_filas": df[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique(),
            "balance": "n/a",
            "parametro_que_alimenta": "segmento_i",
        },
    ]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Admissible feature set
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def admissibility_table(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Per raw column: admissible as an M1 feature, and why or why not.

    This is the Phase 0 artifact that makes controls E1-E3 structural. A column
    is excluded here with a reason, not filtered out later by memory.
    """
    rows = []
    for col in df.columns:
        if col in cfg.EXCLUDED_COLS:
            rows.append((col, "NO", "Excluida por indicacion explicita del diccionario (E1)"))
        elif col == cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE:
            rows.append((col, "NO", "Es el target de M1"))
        elif col in cfg.POST_OUTCOME_COLS:
            rows.append((col, "NO", "Resultado de la visita, no condicion previa (E2/E3) - fuga"))
        elif col == cfg.KEY_CLIENT:
            rows.append((col, "NO", "Clave de agrupacion; identidad no generaliza a clientes nuevos"))
        elif col == cfg.KEY_TIME:
            rows.append((col, "DERIVADA", "No se usa cruda; alimenta features temporales y el split"))
        elif col in cfg.PRICE_COLS:
            rows.append((col, "SI", "Condicion comercial conocida antes de la visita; decision y_jtk"))
        elif col in cfg.PROMO_COLS:
            rows.append((col, "SI", "Condicion comercial conocida antes de la visita; decision z_jt"))
        elif col == "ultima_marca_comprada":
            rows.append((col, "SI", "Lag verificado contra shift(1) por cliente (E4)"))
        elif col == "ultima_cantidad_comprada":
            rows.append((col, "SI", "Lag binario del indicador de compra; el diccionario la nombra mal (E5)"))
        elif col in cfg.DEMOGRAPHIC_COLS:
            rows.append((col, "SI", "Estatica por cliente (D2); segmenta mas de lo que predice (D3)"))
        else:
            rows.append((col, "REVISAR", "No clasificada por el contrato de encuadre"))

    out = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["columna", "admisible", "razon"])
    out.attrs["n_admisibles"] = int((out["admisible"] == "SI").sum())
    out.attrs["n_excluidas"] = int((out["admisible"] == "NO").sum())
    out.attrs["sin_clasificar"] = int((out["admisible"] == "REVISAR").sum())
    return out


def admissible_raw_columns(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list[str]:
    """The raw columns the contract admits as M1 features."""
    table = admissibility_table(df)
    return table.loc[table["admisible"] == "SI", "columna"].tolist()


def forbidden_columns() -> set[str]:
    """Columns that must never appear in the M1 feature matrix."""
    return {
        cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE,
        *cfg.POST_OUTCOME_COLS,
        *cfg.EXCLUDED_COLS,
        cfg.KEY_CLIENT,
        "precio_pagado",
        "gasto",
    }


def assert_feature_matrix_admissible(columns) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Check a built feature matrix against the contract.

    Raises nothing; returns the verdict so the notebook can display it and
    assert on ``attrs['admisible']``.
    """
    present = set(columns)
    violations = sorted(present & forbidden_columns())
    out = pd.DataFrame(
        [
            {
                "verificacion": "ninguna columna prohibida en la matriz de M1",
                "n_features": len(present),
                "violaciones": len(violations),
                "detalle": ", ".join(violations) if violations else "ninguna",
                "estado": "OK" if not violations else "FALLA",
            }
        ]
    )
    out.attrs["admisible"] = not violations
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Validation strategy
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def validation_plan(contract: FramingContract | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The validation strategy, decided now and not negotiable later.

    Each row states the decision and the reason it is forced by this dataset's
    structure rather than chosen by preference.
    """
    contract = contract or FramingContract()
    rows = [
        {
            "aspecto": "Tipo de split",
            "decision": contract.split_tipo,
            "razon": (
                "Con ~117 visitas por cliente, un split aleatorio reparte visitas del mismo "
                "cliente entre train y test e infla todas las metricas de forma irreparable."
            ),
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Frontera del split",
            "decision": f"train: dia <= {contract.split_day} | test: dia > {contract.split_day}",
            "razon": "Deja ~180 dias de test (25%) conservando los 500 clientes y la tasa base.",
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Validacion cruzada en train",
            "decision": contract.cv_estrategia,
            "razon": (
                "TimeSeriesSplit respeta el orden temporal para el tuning; GroupKFold por id "
                "mide generalizacion a clientes nuevos, que con 500 clientes es el riesgo real."
            ),
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Metrica principal",
            "decision": contract.metrica_principal,
            "razon": "Desbalance 25/75: PR-AUC es sensible a la clase positiva, que es la que importa.",
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Metrica prohibida",
            "decision": contract.metrica_prohibida,
            "razon": "Predecir siempre 'no compra' da 75.1% de accuracy y cero valor de negocio.",
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Umbral de decision",
            "decision": contract.umbral_decision,
            "razon": (
                "El umbral optimo iguala margen esperado y costo de contacto: lo fija la "
                "optimizacion P1, no la convencion."
            ),
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Baselines a superar",
            "decision": (
                f"tasa base {contract.baseline_tasa_base} | "
                f"targeting historico lift x{contract.baseline_targeting_lift}"
            ),
            "razon": "Un modelo que no bate el ordenamiento por tasa historica no justifica su existencia.",
        },
        {
            "aspecto": "Preprocesamiento",
            "decision": "Pipeline de scikit-learn con fit solo sobre train (E10)",
            "razon": "Ajustar escalado o codificacion sobre todo el dataset filtra informacion del test.",
        },
    ]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Split freezing
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def split_manifest(df: pd.DataFrame, contract: FramingContract | None = None) -> dict:
    """Describe and fingerprint the frozen split.

    The fingerprint is a hash of the sorted ``(id, dia_visita)`` pairs on each
    side. It changes if the data changes or the boundary moves, which is
    exactly what we want to detect.
    """
    contract = contract or FramingContract()
    day = contract.split_day
    keys = list(contract.clave_unidad)

    manifest = {"split_day": day}
    for name, part in (
        ("train", df[df[cfg.KEY_TIME] <= day]),
        ("test", df[df[cfg.KEY_TIME] > day]),
    ):
        pairs = part[keys].sort_values(keys).astype("int64").to_numpy().tobytes()
        manifest[name] = {
            "filas": int(len(part)),
            "clientes": int(part[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique()),
            "dia_min": int(part[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()),
            "dia_max": int(part[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()),
            "tasa_compra": round(float(part[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()), 4),
            "fingerprint": hashlib.sha256(pairs).hexdigest()[:16],
        }
    manifest["balanceado"] = bool(
        abs(manifest["train"]["tasa_compra"] - manifest["test"]["tasa_compra"]) < 0.01
        and manifest["train"]["clientes"] == manifest["test"]["clientes"]
    )
    return manifest


def split_manifest_table(df: pd.DataFrame, contract: FramingContract | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """The split manifest as a table for display."""
    manifest = split_manifest(df, contract)
    rows = []
    for name in ("train", "test"):
        row = {"split": name, **manifest[name]}
        rows.append(row)
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["split_day"] = manifest["split_day"]
    out.attrs["balanceado"] = manifest["balanceado"]
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Freeze and verify
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def freeze(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    contract: FramingContract | None = None,
    path: Path | None = None,
) -> dict:
    """Write the framing contract and split fingerprint to disk.

    Returns the frozen payload. Call once at the end of Phase 0; later phases
    load it instead of re-deciding.
    """
    contract = contract or FramingContract()
    path = path or FRAMING_PATH
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    admissible = admissibility_table(df)
    payload = {
        "contrato": asdict(contract),
        "unidad_analisis": {
            "valida": bool(verify_unit_of_analysis(df, contract).attrs["unidad_valida"]),
            "filas": int(len(df)),
            "clientes": int(df[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique()),
            "dias": int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].nunique()),
        },
        "features_admisibles": {
            "columnas_crudas_admisibles": admissible_raw_columns(df),
            "n_admisibles": admissible.attrs["n_admisibles"],
            "n_excluidas": admissible.attrs["n_excluidas"],
            "prohibidas": sorted(forbidden_columns()),
        },
        "split": split_manifest(df, contract),
    }

    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    return payload


def load_frozen(path: Path | None = None) -> dict:
    """Load the frozen framing contract."""
    path = path or FRAMING_PATH
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"No hay encuadre congelado en {path}. Ejecutar framing.freeze(df) en la Fase 0."
        )
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def verify_frozen(df: pd.DataFrame, path: Path | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Re-check a dataset against the frozen contract.

    Run this at the top of every later phase. A mismatch means the data, the
    boundary or the feature contract moved, and the models trained before are
    no longer comparable with the ones trained after.
    """
    frozen = load_frozen(path)
    current = split_manifest(df, FramingContract(**{
        k: tuple(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v
        for k, v in frozen["contrato"].items()
    }))

    rows = [
        {
            "verificacion": "frontera del split",
            "congelado": frozen["split"]["split_day"],
            "actual": current["split_day"],
            "estado": "OK" if frozen["split"]["split_day"] == current["split_day"] else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "filas totales",
            "congelado": frozen["unidad_analisis"]["filas"],
            "actual": len(df),
            "estado": "OK" if frozen["unidad_analisis"]["filas"] == len(df) else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "clientes",
            "congelado": frozen["unidad_analisis"]["clientes"],
            "actual": int(df[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique()),
            "estado": "OK" if frozen["unidad_analisis"]["clientes"] == df[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique() else "FALLA",
        },
    ]
    for name in ("train", "test"):
        rows.append(
            {
                "verificacion": f"fingerprint {name}",
                "congelado": frozen["split"][name]["fingerprint"],
                "actual": current[name]["fingerprint"],
                "estado": "OK" if frozen["split"][name]["fingerprint"] == current[name]["fingerprint"] else "FALLA",
            }
        )

    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["coincide"] = bool((out["estado"] == "OK").all())
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Closing the loop: does the modelling phase obey the frozen contract?
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def verify_prepared_against_contract(prepared, path: Path | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Check a :class:`ferreyros.preprocess.PreparedData` against the contract.

    Phase 0 freezes the decisions; Phase 2 builds the matrices. This function
    is what makes the link mechanical instead of a matter of trust: it verifies
    that the split boundary, the row counts, the client partition and the
    feature admissibility of the prepared data are the ones that were frozen.
    """
    frozen = load_frozen(path)
    split = frozen["split"]
    forbidden = set(frozen["features_admisibles"]["prohibidas"])

    input_cols = set(prepared.X_train.columns)
    violations = sorted(input_cols & forbidden)

    rows = [
        {
            "verificacion": "frontera del split",
            "congelado": split["split_day"],
            "actual": prepared.split_day,
            "estado": "OK" if split["split_day"] == prepared.split_day else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "filas de train",
            "congelado": split["train"]["filas"],
            "actual": len(prepared.X_train),
            "estado": "OK" if split["train"]["filas"] == len(prepared.X_train) else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "filas de test",
            "congelado": split["test"]["filas"],
            "actual": len(prepared.X_test),
            "estado": "OK" if split["test"]["filas"] == len(prepared.X_test) else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "tasa de compra en train",
            "congelado": split["train"]["tasa_compra"],
            "actual": round(float(prepared.y_train.mean()), 4),
            "estado": "OK"
            if abs(split["train"]["tasa_compra"] - float(prepared.y_train.mean())) < 1e-4
            else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "tasa de compra en test",
            "congelado": split["test"]["tasa_compra"],
            "actual": round(float(prepared.y_test.mean()), 4),
            "estado": "OK"
            if abs(split["test"]["tasa_compra"] - float(prepared.y_test.mean())) < 1e-4
            else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "clientes presentes en ambos lados",
            "congelado": split["train"]["clientes"],
            "actual": int(prepared.groups_train.nunique()),
            "estado": "OK" if split["train"]["clientes"] == prepared.groups_train.nunique() else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "verificacion": "ninguna columna prohibida en la matriz",
            "congelado": f"{len(forbidden)} prohibidas",
            "actual": ", ".join(violations) if violations else "ninguna",
            "estado": "OK" if not violations else "FALLA",
        },
    ]

    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["obedece_contrato"] = bool((out["estado"] == "OK").all())
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Consolidated Phase 0 report
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def phase0_report(df: pd.DataFrame, contract: FramingContract | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One row per Phase 0 decision, with its verification status."""
    contract = contract or FramingContract()
    unit = verify_unit_of_analysis(df, contract)
    adm = admissibility_table(df)
    split = split_manifest(df, contract)

    rows = [
        {
            "decision": "1. Unidad de analisis",
            "definicion": f"{contract.unidad_analisis} ({' x '.join(contract.clave_unidad)})",
            "estado": "OK" if unit.attrs["unidad_valida"] else "FALLA",
        },
        {
            "decision": "2. Variable objetivo",
            "definicion": f"{contract.target_m1} (M1); {contract.target_m2}/{contract.target_m3} condicionados a compra",
            "estado": "OK",
        },
        {
            "decision": "3. Features admisibles",
            "definicion": f"{adm.attrs['n_admisibles']} columnas crudas admisibles, {adm.attrs['n_excluidas']} excluidas con razon",
            "estado": "OK" if adm.attrs["sin_clasificar"] == 0 else "REVISAR",
        },
        {
            "decision": "4. Estrategia de validacion",
            "definicion": f"{contract.split_tipo}, corte dia {contract.split_day}; {contract.metrica_principal}",
            "estado": "OK" if split["balanceado"] else "REVISAR",
        },
    ]
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["fase0_completa"] = bool((out["estado"] == "OK").all())
    return out


==============================================================================
Fase 1 · EDA
==============================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/eda/__init__.py  (21 lineas)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""EDA modules, one per block of the alignment matrix.

* :mod:`objective`   -- block A: parameters of the objective function (pi, q, lambda, m)
* :mod:`decisions`   -- block B: decision variables (y_jtk, z_jt, x_ijt)
* :mod:`constraints` -- block C: bounds for the constraints (R3-R8)
* :mod:`segments`    -- block D: demographics and the segmentation model M0
* :mod:`dynamics`    -- temporal dynamics: seasonality and trend (Fase 1)
* :mod:`anomalies`   -- coherence, extreme quantities, atypical clients (Fase 1)
"""

from . import anomalies, constraints, decisions, dynamics, objective, segments

__all__ = [
    "objective",
    "decisions",
    "constraints",
    "segments",
    "dynamics",
    "anomalies",
]

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/eda/objective.py  (357 lineas)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""EDA block A -- parameters of the objective function.

Every function here produces the evidence demanded by one row of section 2.1
of ``docs/brief-alineamiento-eda.md``. The objective is

    max  sum_ijt  x_ijt * pi_it * q_ijt * lambda_ijt * m_jk

so this module estimates the empirical shape of ``pi`` (A1-A3), ``q`` (A4-A5),
``lambda`` (A6) and ``m`` (A7).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from .. import config as cfg
from ..data import add_paid_price_and_spend, purchases_only


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A1 -- pi: base rate and temporal stability
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def purchase_rate(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
    """A1: the base rate that every model must beat as a baseline."""
    n = len(df)
    k = int(df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].sum())
    return pd.Series(
        {
            "visitas": n,
            "compras": k,
            "tasa_compra": round(k / n, 4),
            "accuracy_de_predecir_siempre_no_compra": round(1 - k / n, 4),
        }
    )


def purchase_rate_by_period(df: pd.DataFrame, n_periods: int = 4) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A1: stability of the base rate over time.

    A stable rate is what licenses the temporal split: if the rate drifted,
    train and test would not be comparable and the split would need rethinking.
    """
    d = df.copy()
    max_day = int(d[cfg.KEY_TIME].max())
    edges = np.linspace(0, max_day, n_periods + 1).astype(int)
    labels = [f"T{i + 1} (d{edges[i] + 1}-{edges[i + 1]})" for i in range(n_periods)]
    d["periodo"] = pd.cut(d[cfg.KEY_TIME], bins=edges, labels=labels)

    out = d.groupby("periodo", observed=True).agg(
        visitas=(cfg.KEY_CLIENT, "size"),
        clientes=(cfg.KEY_CLIENT, "nunique"),
        tasa_compra=(cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE, "mean"),
    )
    out["tasa_compra"] = out["tasa_compra"].round(4)
    out["desvio_vs_global"] = (out["tasa_compra"] - df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()).round(4)
    return out.reset_index()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A2 -- pi: price sensitivity
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def incidence_by_price_quintile(df: pd.DataFrame, n_bins: int = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A2: purchase rate by quintile of the cheapest price available that day.

    This is the raw elasticity signal: if incidence does not move with price,
    the price decision variable has nothing to optimize against.
    """
    d = df.copy()
    d["precio_min_dia"] = d[cfg.PRICE_COLS].min(axis=1)
    d["quintil_precio_min"] = pd.qcut(d["precio_min_dia"], n_bins, duplicates="drop")

    out = d.groupby("quintil_precio_min", observed=True).agg(
        visitas=(cfg.KEY_CLIENT, "size"),
        precio_min_medio=("precio_min_dia", "mean"),
        tasa_compra=(cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE, "mean"),
    ).reset_index()
    out["precio_min_medio"] = out["precio_min_medio"].round(3)
    out["tasa_compra"] = out["tasa_compra"].round(4)

    first, last = out["tasa_compra"].iloc[0], out["tasa_compra"].iloc[-1]
    out.attrs["caida_relativa_pct"] = round(100 * (1 - last / first), 1)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        f"La incidencia cae de {first:.4f} a {last:.4f} entre el quintil mas barato "
        f"y el mas caro: {out.attrs['caida_relativa_pct']}% de caida relativa."
    )
    return out


def incidence_by_brand_price_quintile(df: pd.DataFrame, n_bins: int = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A2 (detail): own-price sensitivity of each brand's purchase incidence.

    Uses each brand's own price distribution, so the result is comparable
    across brands despite their very different price levels.
    """
    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        d = df.copy()
        col = f"precio_marca_{j}"
        d["q"] = pd.qcut(d[col], n_bins, labels=False, duplicates="drop")
        g = d.groupby("q", observed=True).agg(
            precio_medio=(col, "mean"),
            tasa_compra=(cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE, "mean"),
        )
        if len(g) < 2:
            continue
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "precio_q1": round(float(g["precio_medio"].iloc[0]), 3),
                "precio_q_ultimo": round(float(g["precio_medio"].iloc[-1]), 3),
                "tasa_q1": round(float(g["tasa_compra"].iloc[0]), 4),
                "tasa_q_ultimo": round(float(g["tasa_compra"].iloc[-1]), 4),
                "caida_relativa_pct": round(
                    100 * (1 - g["tasa_compra"].iloc[-1] / g["tasa_compra"].iloc[0]), 1
                ),
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A3 -- pi: promotion sensitivity
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def incidence_by_n_promos(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A3: purchase rate by how many brands are on promotion that day."""
    d = df.copy()
    d["n_promos_dia"] = d[cfg.PROMO_COLS].sum(axis=1)

    out = d.groupby("n_promos_dia").agg(
        visitas=(cfg.KEY_CLIENT, "size"),
        tasa_compra=(cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE, "mean"),
    ).reset_index()
    out["tasa_compra"] = out["tasa_compra"].round(4)

    base = out.loc[out["n_promos_dia"] == 0, "tasa_compra"].iloc[0]
    out["lift_vs_sin_promo"] = (out["tasa_compra"] / base).round(3)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "El efecto no es monotono: crece hasta 2 marcas en promocion y luego cae. "
        "Promocionar mas no es promocionar mejor."
    )
    return out


def incidence_by_promo_per_brand(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A3: purchase rate with and without promotion, brand by brand.

    This is a different question from A5 and the two together are what the
    objective function needs. A5 asks how the *share* of a brand moves when it
    is discounted, conditional on a purchase happening: that is cannibalization
    between brands. This asks whether the discount makes a purchase happen at
    all: that is market expansion. A promotion that only moves share pays for
    units that would have been sold anyway.

    The difference of the two rates is tested against its standard error, so a
    brand is only credited with expanding the market when the gap survives the
    sample size behind it.
    """
    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        on = df[f"promo_marca_{j}"] == 1
        n_on, n_off = int(on.sum()), int((~on).sum())
        r_on = float(df.loc[on, cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()) if n_on else np.nan
        r_off = float(df.loc[~on, cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean())
        se = np.sqrt(r_on * (1 - r_on) / n_on + r_off * (1 - r_off) / n_off) if n_on else np.nan
        z = (r_on - r_off) / se if n_on and se else np.nan
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "dias_promo": int(df.loc[on, cfg.KEY_TIME].nunique()),
                "visitas_con_promo": n_on,
                "tasa_con_promo": round(r_on, 4),
                "tasa_sin_promo": round(r_off, 4),
                "delta_pp": round(100 * (r_on - r_off), 2),
                "lift": round(r_on / r_off, 3) if r_off else np.nan,
                "z": round(float(z), 2) if n_on else np.nan,
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out["efecto"] = np.where(
        out["z"].abs() <= 2,
        "no distinguible del ruido",
        np.where(out["delta_pp"] > 0, "expande el mercado", "contrae la incidencia"),
    )

    expanden = out.loc[out["efecto"] == "expande el mercado", "marca"].tolist()
    out.attrs["marcas_que_expanden"] = expanden
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "{} de 5 marcas mueven la incidencia por encima del ruido ({}). El resto "
        "redistribuye share sin crear compras: su descuento se paga con unidades que "
        "se habrian vendido igual. La funcion objetivo necesita las dos lecturas, "
        "porque el termino de promocion resta costo siempre y suma ingreso solo si "
        "hay compra incremental."
    ).format(
        len(expanden),
        "marcas " + ", ".join(str(m) for m in expanden) if expanden else "ninguna",
    )
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A4, A5 -- q: brand choice and substitution
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def brand_share(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A4: brand share conditional on purchase -- the universe of model M2."""
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    out = comp.groupby(cfg.TARGET_BRAND).agg(
        compras=(cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, "size"),
        unidades=(cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, "sum"),
        precio_pagado_medio=("precio_pagado", "mean"),
        cantidad_media=(cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, "mean"),
        ingreso=("gasto", "sum"),
    ).reset_index()

    out["share_compras_pct"] = (100 * out["compras"] / out["compras"].sum()).round(1)
    out["share_ingreso_pct"] = (100 * out["ingreso"] / out["ingreso"].sum()).round(1)
    for col in ("precio_pagado_medio", "cantidad_media", "ingreso"):
        out[col] = out[col].round(2)

    out.attrs["universo"] = (
        f"Condicional a compra: {len(comp):,} filas de {len(df):,}. "
        "Este es el universo de entrenamiento de M2 y M3 (control E12)."
    )
    return out


def promo_lift_on_brand_share(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A5: how much each brand's share rises when it is on promotion.

    This is the central finding of the EDA: the brands that are promoted least
    are the ones that respond most. It is the arbitrage the optimizer exploits.
    """
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        on_promo = comp[f"promo_marca_{j}"] == 1
        share_on = float((comp.loc[on_promo, cfg.TARGET_BRAND] == j).mean()) if on_promo.any() else np.nan
        share_off = float((comp.loc[~on_promo, cfg.TARGET_BRAND] == j).mean())
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "frecuencia_promo": round(float(df[f"promo_marca_{j}"].mean()), 4),
                "share_con_promo": round(share_on, 3),
                "share_sin_promo": round(share_off, 3),
                "lift": round(share_on / share_off, 2) if share_off > 0 else np.nan,
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out["respuesta_vs_inversion"] = np.where(
        (out["lift"] > out["lift"].median()) & (out["frecuencia_promo"] < out["frecuencia_promo"].median()),
        "SUB-PROMOCIONADA (alta respuesta, baja inversion)",
        np.where(
            (out["lift"] < out["lift"].median()) & (out["frecuencia_promo"] > out["frecuencia_promo"].median()),
            "SOBRE-PROMOCIONADA (baja respuesta, alta inversion)",
            "alineada",
        ),
    )
    return out


def brand_switching_matrix(df: pd.DataFrame, normalize: bool = True) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A5: transition matrix from the previously bought brand to the current one.

    Quantifies loyalty (diagonal) versus substitution (off-diagonal), which is
    what constraint-free cross-price effects in model M2 must reproduce.
    """
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    comp = comp[comp["ultima_marca_comprada"] > 0]
    mat = pd.crosstab(
        comp["ultima_marca_comprada"],
        comp[cfg.TARGET_BRAND],
        normalize="index" if normalize else False,
    )
    mat.index.name = "marca_anterior"
    mat.columns.name = "marca_actual"
    return mat.round(3) if normalize else mat


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A6 -- lambda: quantity given purchase
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def quantity_distribution(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A6: distribution of ``cantidad`` conditional on a purchase."""
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    counts = comp[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY].value_counts().sort_index()
    out = counts.rename("compras").reset_index()
    out.columns = ["cantidad", "compras"]
    out["pct"] = (100 * out["compras"] / out["compras"].sum()).round(1)
    out["pct_acumulado"] = out["pct"].cumsum().round(1)
    out.attrs["resumen"] = comp[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY].describe().round(2).to_dict()
    return out


def quantity_summary_by_brand(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A6: expected quantity per brand -- this is ``lambda_ijt`` at brand level."""
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    out = comp.groupby(cfg.TARGET_BRAND)[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY].agg(
        compras="size", media="mean", mediana="median", p90=lambda s: s.quantile(0.9), maximo="max"
    ).reset_index()
    out["media"] = out["media"].round(2)
    out["p90"] = out["p90"].round(1)
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A7 -- m: revenue, and the missing cost parameter
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def revenue_by_brand(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A7: observed revenue per brand, with the cost gap stated explicitly."""
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    out = comp.groupby(cfg.TARGET_BRAND).agg(
        unidades=(cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, "sum"),
        ingreso=("gasto", "sum"),
        precio_medio=("precio_pagado", "mean"),
    ).reset_index()
    out["share_ingreso_pct"] = (100 * out["ingreso"] / out["ingreso"].sum()).round(1)
    out["ingreso"] = out["ingreso"].round(2)
    out["precio_medio"] = out["precio_medio"].round(2)
    out["margen_unitario"] = "REQUIERE SUPUESTO (c_j no esta en el dato)"

    out.attrs["brecha"] = (
        "El margen m_jk = p_jk - c_j no es calculable: el dataset no contiene costos. "
        "La optimizacion se entrega con analisis de sensibilidad sobre c_j (control C2)."
    )
    out.attrs["ingreso_total"] = round(float(comp["gasto"].sum()), 2)
    return out


def missing_business_parameters() -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A7 / C2: the parameters the optimization needs and the data cannot give.

    Declaring this table is itself an acceptance criterion: it delimits the
    honest scope of the solution.
    """
    rows = [
        ("c_j", "Costo unitario por marca", "No", "Supuesto de margen bruto % por marca + sensibilidad"),
        ("a", "Costo de contacto / impulso comercial", "No", "Supuesto; define el umbral de decision de P1"),
        ("d_j", "Costo de un periodo de promocion", "No", "Supuesto; acota el calendario de P2"),
        ("B", "Presupuesto de ejecucion", "No", "Input de negocio; restriccion R2"),
        ("F_i", "Tope de contactos por cliente", "Parcial", "Acotable con la frecuencia de visita observada"),
        ("Cap_jt", "Capacidad / inventario por marca", "Parcial", "Acotable con unidades/dia observadas"),
        ("rho", "Margen porcentual minimo", "No", "Politica comercial; restriccion R6"),
    ]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["parametro", "descripcion", "esta_en_el_dato", "tratamiento"])

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/eda/decisions.py  (340 lineas)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""EDA block B -- decision variables of the optimization problem.

Produces the evidence demanded by section 2.2 of
``docs/brief-alineamiento-eda.md``:

* ``y_jtk`` -- the discrete price ladder (B1) and its dynamics (B2)
* ``z_jt``  -- the promotional calendar (B3)
* ``x_ijt`` -- targeting: client heterogeneity (B4), value concentration (B5)
               and the baseline the model must beat (B6)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from .. import config as cfg
from ..data import purchases_only


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B1 -- y_jtk: the discrete price ladder
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def price_ladder(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B1: enumerate the price points actually observed for each brand.

    This table is what turns the MINLP into a tractable MILP: the price is not
    a continuous variable, it is a menu of 21 to 44 already-observed points.
    """
    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        col = f"precio_marca_{j}"
        values = np.sort(df[col].unique())
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "n_puntos_precio": len(values),
                "precio_min": round(float(values[0]), 2),
                "precio_max": round(float(values[-1]), 2),
                "precio_medio": round(float(df[col].mean()), 2),
                "rango_relativo_pct": round(100 * (values[-1] / values[0] - 1), 1),
                "paso_mediano": round(float(np.median(np.diff(values))), 3) if len(values) > 1 else np.nan,
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        f"El espacio de decision de precio es finito: "
        f"{' x '.join(str(n) for n in out['n_puntos_precio'])} combinaciones por periodo. "
        "Discretizar permite precalcular pi, q y lambda por escenario y resolver un MILP."
    )
    return out


def price_points(df: pd.DataFrame, brand: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """B1: the sorted menu ``K_j`` of price points for one brand."""
    return np.sort(df[f"precio_marca_{brand}"].unique())


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B2 -- y_jtk: price dynamics
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def daily_price_series(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B2: one row per day with the five prices, for the trajectory plot."""
    return df.groupby(cfg.KEY_TIME)[cfg.PRICE_COLS].first().reset_index()


def price_correlation(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B2: do brand prices move together?

    High correlation means the brands are repriced jointly, so the optimizer
    cannot treat each brand's price as an independent lever in practice.
    """
    daily = daily_price_series(df)
    return daily[cfg.PRICE_COLS].corr().round(3)


def price_overlap_check(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """C3 / B2: does the brand price architecture hold strictly?

    Means are ordered, but ranges overlap. That makes constraint R7 (strict
    price ordering) a modeling decision rather than a fact of the data.
    """
    ladder = price_ladder(df).set_index("marca")
    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS[:-1]:
        nxt = j + 1
        overlap = ladder.loc[j, "precio_max"] > ladder.loc[nxt, "precio_min"]
        rows.append(
            {
                "par": f"marca {j} vs marca {nxt}",
                "max_marca_inferior": ladder.loc[j, "precio_max"],
                "min_marca_superior": ladder.loc[nxt, "precio_min"],
                "se_solapan": bool(overlap),
                "medias_ordenadas": bool(ladder.loc[j, "precio_medio"] < ladder.loc[nxt, "precio_medio"]),
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "Las medias estan ordenadas pero los rangos se solapan. El ordenamiento estricto "
        "de precios (R7) es una decision de modelado para proteger el posicionamiento, "
        "no un hecho del dato: se implementa activable y se reporta su costo."
    )
    return out


def relative_price_gap(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B2: relative price of each brand against the daily average and the cheapest.

    The absolute price of a brand says little, because all five move: what a
    client sees is the **gap**. Two readings come out of this table. The level
    of ``precio_relativo`` is the brand's position, which is what feature
    ``precio_relativo_j`` encodes. Its dispersion over time is whether that
    position actually moves -- if the gaps were frozen, no pricing lever would
    exist for the optimizer and own and cross elasticity would be
    unidentifiable from observational data.
    """
    daily = daily_price_series(df)
    media_dia = daily[cfg.PRICE_COLS].mean(axis=1)
    min_dia = daily[cfg.PRICE_COLS].min(axis=1)

    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        p = daily[f"precio_marca_{j}"]
        rel = p / media_dia
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "precio_medio": round(float(p.mean()), 3),
                "precio_relativo_medio": round(float(rel.mean()), 3),
                "precio_relativo_min": round(float(rel.min()), 3),
                "precio_relativo_max": round(float(rel.max()), 3),
                "desvio_std_relativo": round(float(rel.std()), 4),
                "brecha_vs_mas_barata": round(float((p - min_dia).mean()), 3),
                "pct_dias_mas_barata": round(100 * float((p <= min_dia + 1e-12).mean()), 1),
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)

    lider = int(out.loc[out["pct_dias_mas_barata"].idxmax(), "marca"])
    mas_movil = int(out.loc[out["desvio_std_relativo"].idxmax(), "marca"])
    out.attrs["marca_mas_barata"] = lider
    out.attrs["marca_mas_movil"] = mas_movil
    out.attrs["dispersion_media"] = round(float(out["desvio_std_relativo"].mean()), 4)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "La marca {} es la mas barata el {:.1f}% de los dias y la marca {} es la que "
        "mueve mas su posicion relativa (desvio {:.4f}). La dispersion media del precio "
        "relativo es {:.4f}: las brechas se mueven, asi que hay palanca de precio que "
        "optimizar y variacion suficiente para identificar elasticidades."
    ).format(
        lider,
        float(out["pct_dias_mas_barata"].max()),
        mas_movil,
        float(out["desvio_std_relativo"].max()),
        out.attrs["dispersion_media"],
    )
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B3 -- z_jt: the promotional calendar
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def promo_calendar_profile(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B3: how often each brand is promoted, and in what blocks.

    The observed frequency bounds ``T_j`` in constraint R5.
    """
    daily = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_TIME)[cfg.PROMO_COLS].first()
    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        serie = daily[f"promo_marca_{j}"]
        blocks = (serie.diff() == 1).sum() + int(serie.iloc[0] == 1)
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "dias_en_promo": int(serie.sum()),
                "frecuencia_promo": round(float(serie.mean()), 4),
                "n_bloques_promo": int(blocks),
                "duracion_media_bloque": round(float(serie.sum() / blocks), 1) if blocks else np.nan,
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def promo_vs_response(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B3: confront promotional investment with promotional response.

    The core misalignment: brands 1 and 2 absorb a third of the promotional
    days and barely respond; brands 3 and 5 are almost never promoted and
    respond most.
    """
    from .objective import promo_lift_on_brand_share

    lift = promo_lift_on_brand_share(df).set_index("marca")
    revenue = purchases_only(df).groupby(cfg.TARGET_BRAND)["gasto"].sum()
    share = (100 * revenue / revenue.sum()).round(1)

    out = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "frecuencia_promo": lift["frecuencia_promo"],
            "lift_share_con_promo": lift["lift"],
            "share_ingreso_pct": share,
            "diagnostico": lift["respuesta_vs_inversion"],
        }
    ).reset_index()
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "Hipotesis de arbitraje: reasignar dias de promocion de las marcas 1-2 "
        "hacia 3 y 5. El optimizador (P2) cuantifica el margen liberado."
    )
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B4 -- x_ijt: client heterogeneity
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def client_profile(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B4: one row per client with behaviour and demographics.

    Demographics are taken with ``first`` because control D2 proves they are
    invariant within a client.
    """
    comp_flag = df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE]
    agg = {
        "visitas": (cfg.KEY_TIME, "size"),
        "compras": (cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE, "sum"),
        "primer_dia": (cfg.KEY_TIME, "min"),
        "ultimo_dia": (cfg.KEY_TIME, "max"),
    }
    for col in cfg.DEMOGRAPHIC_COLS:
        agg[col] = (col, "first")

    out = df.assign(_flag=comp_flag).groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT).agg(**agg)
    out["tasa_compra"] = (out["compras"] / out["visitas"]).round(4)

    if "gasto" in df.columns:
        spend = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)["gasto"].sum()
    else:
        spend = purchases_only(df).groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)["gasto"].sum()
    out["gasto_total"] = spend.reindex(out.index).fillna(0).round(2)
    out["ticket_medio"] = (out["gasto_total"] / out["compras"].replace(0, np.nan)).round(2)
    return out.reset_index()


def heterogeneity_summary(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B4: dispersion of purchase rate across clients.

    Without this dispersion the targeting problem would have no solution worth
    computing: every client would be equally worth contacting.
    """
    prof = client_profile(df)
    desc = prof[["visitas", "compras", "tasa_compra", "gasto_total"]].describe().round(3)
    desc.attrs["lectura"] = (
        f"La tasa de compra va de {prof['tasa_compra'].min():.3f} a "
        f"{prof['tasa_compra'].max():.3f} con mediana {prof['tasa_compra'].median():.3f}. "
        "Esa dispersion es la razon de existir del problema de targeting."
    )
    return desc


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B5 -- x_ijt: value concentration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def pareto_concentration(df: pd.DataFrame, quantiles=(0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5)) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B5: share of revenue held by the top-N% of clients."""
    prof = client_profile(df)
    spend = prof.set_index(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)["gasto_total"].sort_values(ascending=False)
    total = spend.sum()

    rows = []
    for q in quantiles:
        n = int(len(spend) * q)
        rows.append(
            {
                "top_pct_clientes": int(q * 100),
                "n_clientes": n,
                "ingreso": round(float(spend.head(n).sum()), 2),
                "share_ingreso_pct": round(100 * float(spend.head(n).sum()) / total, 1),
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def pareto_curve(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B5: cumulative revenue share by cumulative client share, for plotting."""
    prof = client_profile(df)
    spend = prof["gasto_total"].sort_values(ascending=False).to_numpy()
    cum = np.cumsum(spend) / spend.sum()
    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "pct_clientes": 100 * np.arange(1, len(spend) + 1) / len(spend),
            "pct_ingreso_acumulado": 100 * cum,
        }
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B6 -- x_ijt: the baseline the model must beat
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def targeting_baseline_lift(df: pd.DataFrame, deciles=(0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5)) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """B6: lift obtained by ranking clients on their historical purchase rate.

    This needs no model. It is the floor the ML model has to clear to justify
    its existence, and the comparison point for P1's objective value.
    """
    prof = client_profile(df)
    base = float(df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean())
    ranked = prof.sort_values("tasa_compra", ascending=False)

    rows = []
    for q in deciles:
        n = int(len(ranked) * q)
        top = ranked.head(n)
        rows.append(
            {
                "top_pct_clientes": int(q * 100),
                "n_clientes": n,
                "tasa_compra_del_grupo": round(float(top["tasa_compra"].mean()), 4),
                "lift_vs_base": round(float(top["tasa_compra"].mean()) / base, 2),
                "share_ingreso_pct": round(100 * float(top["gasto_total"].sum()) / prof["gasto_total"].sum(), 1),
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["tasa_base"] = round(base, 4)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        f"Ordenar por tasa historica da lift x{out['lift_vs_base'].iloc[0]} en el decil superior "
        "sin usar ningun modelo. M1 debe superarlo."
    )
    return out

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/eda/constraints.py  (194 lineas)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""EDA block C -- bounds for the optimization constraints.

Produces the evidence demanded by section 2.3 of
``docs/brief-alineamiento-eda.md``. Each function bounds one constraint of the
problem with observed data, so the MILP is parameterized from evidence rather
than from assumption wherever that is possible.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from .. import config as cfg
from ..data import purchases_only
from .decisions import price_overlap_check, promo_calendar_profile  # re-exported


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# C4 -- R8: capacity / inventory
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def capacity_bounds(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """C4: observed units sold per brand, per day -- bounds ``Cap_jt`` in R8."""
    comp = purchases_only(df)
    n_days = int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].nunique())

    daily_units = (
        comp.groupby([cfg.KEY_TIME, cfg.TARGET_BRAND])[cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY]
        .sum()
        .unstack(fill_value=0)
    )

    rows = []
    for j in cfg.BRANDS:
        serie = daily_units[j] if j in daily_units.columns else pd.Series(0, index=daily_units.index)
        rows.append(
            {
                "marca": j,
                "unidades_totales": int(serie.sum()),
                "unidades_por_dia_media": round(float(serie.sum() / n_days), 2),
                "unidades_por_dia_p95": round(float(serie.quantile(0.95)), 1),
                "unidades_por_dia_max": int(serie.max()),
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "La media acota la capacidad en regimen; el p95 y el maximo acotan el pico. "
        "Cap_jt debe fijarse sobre el pico, no sobre la media."
    )
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# C5 -- R3, R4: contact fatigue and operational scale
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def fatigue_bounds(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """C5: visit frequency per client and per day -- bounds ``F_i`` in R4.

    R3 (one offer per client per period) is feasible by construction; R4 needs
    a realistic cap, and the observed visit frequency is its natural anchor.
    """
    per_client = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT).size()
    per_day = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_TIME).size()
    unique_clients_per_day = df.groupby(cfg.KEY_TIME)[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique()
    horizon = int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].nunique())

    rows = [
        ("visitas por cliente (horizonte completo)", per_client.mean(), per_client.min(), per_client.max()),
        ("visitas por dia", per_day.mean(), per_day.min(), per_day.max()),
        ("clientes distintos por dia", unique_clients_per_day.mean(),
         unique_clients_per_day.min(), unique_clients_per_day.max()),
    ]
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["metrica", "media", "min", "max"])
    out["media"] = out["media"].round(1)
    out.attrs["horizonte_dias"] = horizon
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        f"Con {per_client.mean():.0f} visitas por cliente en {horizon} dias, el cliente aparece "
        "cada ~6 dias. F_i debe fijarse muy por debajo de esa frecuencia para no saturar."
    )
    return out


def visit_interval_profile(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """C5: distribution of the gap in days between consecutive visits."""
    d = df.sort_values([cfg.KEY_CLIENT, cfg.KEY_TIME])
    gaps = d.groupby(cfg.KEY_CLIENT)[cfg.KEY_TIME].diff().dropna()
    desc = gaps.describe(percentiles=[0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9]).round(2)
    return desc.rename("dias_entre_visitas").to_frame().reset_index(names="estadistico")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# C1 -- R5: promotional-day budget
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def promo_budget_bounds(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """C1: observed promotional days per brand -- bounds ``T_j`` in R5."""
    out = promo_calendar_profile(df)
    horizon = int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].nunique())
    out["horizonte_dias"] = horizon
    out["T_j_observado"] = out["dias_en_promo"]
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        "T_j se fija a partir de lo observado. El total de dias-promocion del horizonte "
        f"({int(out['dias_en_promo'].sum())}) es el presupuesto a reasignar, no a aumentar."
    )
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# C2 -- R6: margin floor (the honest gap)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def margin_floor_gap() -> pd.DataFrame:
    """C2: state that R6 cannot be parameterized from the data.

    Returning this table is the acceptance criterion for C2: the evaluation
    requires the gap to be declared, not silently assumed away.
    """
    rows = [
        {
            "restriccion": "R6 piso de margen",
            "parametro_requerido": "c_j (costo unitario), rho (margen minimo)",
            "disponible_en_el_dato": "No",
            "tratamiento": (
                "Parametrizar como supuesto de margen bruto por marca y entregar el "
                "resultado como analisis de sensibilidad, no como cifra unica."
            ),
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R2 presupuesto",
            "parametro_requerido": "a (costo de contacto), d_j (costo de promo), B",
            "disponible_en_el_dato": "No",
            "tratamiento": "Input de negocio; el umbral de decision de P1 depende de a.",
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R7 arquitectura de marcas",
            "parametro_requerido": "Ordenamiento de precios exigido",
            "disponible_en_el_dato": "Parcial (medias ordenadas, rangos solapados)",
            "tratamiento": "Restriccion activable; se reporta el costo de imponerla.",
        },
    ]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Consolidated view
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def constraint_parameter_summary(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One row per constraint: is it bounded by evidence, or by assumption?"""
    cap = capacity_bounds(df)
    fat = fatigue_bounds(df)
    promo = promo_budget_bounds(df)

    rows = [
        {
            "restriccion": "R3 una oferta por cliente y periodo",
            "fuente": "estructura del problema",
            "parametrizada_con": "factible por construccion",
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R4 fatiga de contacto (F_i)",
            "fuente": "evidencia",
            "parametrizada_con": f"{fat.loc[0, 'media']:.0f} visitas/cliente observadas",
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R5 dias de promocion (T_j)",
            "fuente": "evidencia",
            "parametrizada_con": f"{int(promo['dias_en_promo'].sum())} dias-promo observados",
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R6 piso de margen",
            "fuente": "SUPUESTO",
            "parametrizada_con": "c_j y rho no estan en el dato",
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R7 arquitectura de marcas",
            "fuente": "decision de modelado",
            "parametrizada_con": "rangos de precio solapados",
        },
        {
            "restriccion": "R8 capacidad (Cap_jt)",
            "fuente": "evidencia",
            "parametrizada_con": f"pico diario hasta {int(cap['unidades_por_dia_max'].max())} unidades",
        },
    ]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHIVO: src/ferreyros/eda/segments.py  (184 lineas)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""EDA block D -- demographics and the segmentation model M0.

Produces the evidence demanded by section 2.4 of
``docs/brief-alineamiento-eda.md``. The central conclusion this block must
support is D3: demographics *segment* but do not *predict*, so they belong in
the clustering and in commercial action, not in the predictive core.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from .. import config as cfg
from .decisions import client_profile


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# D1 -- demographic profile of the 500 clients
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def demographic_profile(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """D1: distribution of each demographic variable at client level.

    Measured over the 500 clients, not the 58,693 visits: using visit level
    would weight each client by how often they shop.
    """
    prof = client_profile(df)
    rows = []
    for col in cfg.DEMOGRAPHIC_COLS:
        serie = prof[col]
        rows.append(
            {
                "variable": col,
                "n_niveles": int(serie.nunique()),
                "tipo": "continua" if serie.nunique() > 10 else "categorica/ordinal",
                "media": round(float(serie.mean()), 2),
                "min": round(float(serie.min()), 2),
                "p50": round(float(serie.median()), 2),
                "max": round(float(serie.max()), 2),
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def categorical_breakdown(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """D1: level-by-level counts for the categorical demographics."""
    prof = client_profile(df)
    labels = {
        "genero": cfg.GENDER_LABELS,
        "estado_civil": cfg.MARITAL_LABELS,
        "nivel_educacion": cfg.EDUCATION_LABELS,
        "ocupacion": cfg.OCCUPATION_LABELS,
    }
    rows = []
    for col, mapping in labels.items():
        counts = prof[col].value_counts().sort_index()
        for level, n in counts.items():
            rows.append(
                {
                    "variable": col,
                    "nivel": mapping.get(level, str(level)),
                    "clientes": int(n),
                    "pct_clientes": round(100 * n / len(prof), 1),
                }
            )
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# D3 -- predictive power of demographics
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def purchase_rate_by_demographic(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """D3: purchase rate by demographic level, measured at visit level.

    The spread across levels is what the acceptance criterion looks at: if it
    is narrow, demographics cannot carry the prediction.
    """
    categorical = ["genero", "estado_civil", "nivel_educacion", "ocupacion"]
    labels = {
        "genero": cfg.GENDER_LABELS,
        "estado_civil": cfg.MARITAL_LABELS,
        "nivel_educacion": cfg.EDUCATION_LABELS,
        "ocupacion": cfg.OCCUPATION_LABELS,
    }
    rows = []
    for col in categorical:
        g = df.groupby(col)[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].agg(["mean", "size"])
        for level, (rate, n) in g.iterrows():
            rows.append(
                {
                    "variable": col,
                    "nivel": labels[col].get(level, str(level)),
                    "visitas": int(n),
                    "tasa_compra": round(float(rate), 4),
                }
            )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    base = float(df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean())
    out["lift_vs_base"] = (out["tasa_compra"] / base).round(2)
    return out


def demographic_spread(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """D3: for each demographic variable, the spread of purchase rate it explains.

    Compared against the client-level spread (0.046 to 0.922), a narrow
    demographic spread is the evidence for "segments but does not predict".
    """
    rates = purchase_rate_by_demographic(df)
    rows = []
    for col, grp in rates.groupby("variable"):
        rows.append(
            {
                "variable": col,
                "tasa_min": grp["tasa_compra"].min(),
                "tasa_max": grp["tasa_compra"].max(),
                "amplitud": round(float(grp["tasa_compra"].max() - grp["tasa_compra"].min()), 4),
                "ratio_max_min": round(float(grp["tasa_compra"].max() / grp["tasa_compra"].min()), 2),
            }
        )
    out = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("amplitud", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)

    prof = client_profile(df)
    client_spread = float(prof["tasa_compra"].max() - prof["tasa_compra"].min())
    out.attrs["amplitud_entre_clientes"] = round(client_spread, 4)
    out.attrs["lectura"] = (
        f"La mayor amplitud demografica es {out['amplitud'].iloc[0]:.4f}, frente a "
        f"{client_spread:.4f} de amplitud entre clientes individuales. "
        "La demografia segmenta pero no predice: el poder predictivo esta en el "
        "comportamiento y el contexto comercial."
    )
    return out


def continuous_demographic_bins(df: pd.DataFrame, n_bins: int = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """D3: purchase rate by bin of age and annual income."""
    rows = []
