"""Export everything the dashboard and the deliverables need, in one pass.

``dashboard/index.html`` is a static page: it reads ``dashboard/data.json`` and
nothing else, so it opens offline with a double click. That means every
scenario the page lets you explore has to be **precomputed here** -- the budget
frontier, the cost-ratio grid, the three margin assumptions, the price plans
with and without R7, the propensity curve of each brand.

The page says so in a visible notice. Interactivity over precomputed scenarios
is honest; a static page that implied live scoring would not be.

    uv run python -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'src'); from ferreyros import dashboard; dashboard.export_all()"
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from . import config as cfg
from . import data as data_mod
from . import framing
from . import models as mdl
from . import prescriptive as px
from . import segmentation as seg_mod
from .eda import anomalies, decisions, dynamics, objective, segments

DASHBOARD_DIR = cfg.PROJECT_ROOT / "dashboard"
FIGURES_DIR = cfg.PROJECT_ROOT / "docs" / "figuras"
ARTIFACTS_DIR = cfg.PROJECT_ROOT / "artifacts"


def _records(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list[dict]:
    """A DataFrame as JSON-safe records, index included when it carries meaning."""
    out = df.reset_index() if df.index.name else df
    return json.loads(out.to_json(orient="records", double_precision=6))


def _frozen(nombre: str) -> dict:
    ruta = ARTIFACTS_DIR / nombre
    return json.loads(ruta.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if ruta.exists() else {}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# One computation pass, shared by the dashboard and the figures
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def compute(verbose: bool = True) -> dict:
    """Run every phase once and keep the tables both outputs need."""
    def log(msg: str) -> None:
        if verbose:
            print(msg, flush=True)

    R: dict = {}
    log("· cargando datos y verificando contratos")
    df = data_mod.add_paid_price_and_spend(data_mod.load_raw())
    modelo_m0 = seg_mod.fit(df, k=3)
    assert framing.verify_frozen(df).attrs["coincide"], "encuadre congelado no coincide"
    assert seg_mod.verify_frozen(modelo_m0).attrs["coincide"], "segmentos congelados no coinciden"
    R["df"], R["m0"] = df, modelo_m0

    log("· EDA")
    R["tasa"] = objective.purchase_rate(df)
    R["brand_share"] = objective.brand_share(df)
    R["promo_lift"] = objective.promo_lift_on_brand_share(df)
    R["promo_incidencia"] = objective.incidence_by_promo_per_brand(df)
    R["ladder"] = decisions.price_ladder(df)
    R["gap"] = decisions.relative_price_gap(df)
    R["baseline"] = decisions.targeting_baseline_lift(data_mod.add_paid_price_and_spend(df))
    R["temporal"] = dynamics.temporal_dynamics_summary(df)
    R["anomalias"] = anomalies.anomaly_summary(df)
    R["demografia"] = segments.purchase_rate_by_demographic(df)

    log("· segmentos (M0)")
    R["perfil_seg"] = seg_mod.segment_profile(modelo_m0, df)
    R["comportamiento_seg"] = seg_mod.segment_behaviour(modelo_m0, df)
    R["marcas_seg"] = seg_mod.segment_brand_share(modelo_m0, df)
    R["promo_seg"] = seg_mod.segment_promo_response(modelo_m0, df)
    R["pca"] = seg_mod.pca_projection(modelo_m0, df)
    R["seleccion_k"] = seg_mod.select_k(df)

    log("· modelos (M1/M2/M3)")
    prep = seg_mod.prepare_with_segment(modelo_m0, df)
    X_train, X_test = mdl.drop_features(prep.X_train, prep.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)
    pipe_m1, proba = mdl.fit_final(
        mdl.m1_candidates()["1 · logistica"], X_train, prep.y_train, X_test, {"C": 0.03}
    )
    R["m1_test"] = mdl.evaluate_binary(prep.y_test, proba, "logistica C=0.03")
    R["lift"] = mdl.lift_table(prep.y_test, proba)
    R["contra_baseline"] = mdl.baseline_policy_lift(X_test, prep.y_test, proba)
    R["umbral"] = mdl.threshold_by_cost(prep.y_test, proba)
    R["shap"] = mdl.shap_global(pipe_m1, X_test, top=12)
    R["curva_pr"] = _pr_curve(prep.y_test, proba)
    R["calibracion"] = _calibration(prep.y_test, proba)
    R["y_test"], R["proba_m1"] = prep.y_test, proba

    cond2 = mdl.prepare_conditional(
        cfg.TARGET_BRAND, df=seg_mod.attach_segment(df, modelo_m0), extra=["segmento"]
    )
    cond3 = mdl.prepare_conditional(
        cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, df=seg_mod.attach_segment(df, modelo_m0), extra=["segmento"]
    )
    X2tr, X2te = mdl.drop_features(cond2.X_train, cond2.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)
    X3tr, X3te = mdl.drop_features(cond3.X_train, cond3.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)
    pipe_m2 = mdl.pipeline(mdl.m2_candidates()["2 · random forest"], X2tr).fit(X2tr, cond2.y_train)
    pipe_m3 = mdl.pipeline(mdl.m3_candidates()["1 · poisson"], X3tr).fit(X3tr, cond3.y_train)
    R["m2_test"] = mdl.evaluate_multiclass(cond2.y_test, pipe_m2.predict_proba(X2te), pipe_m2.classes_)
    R["m3_test"] = mdl.evaluate_count(
        cond3.y_test, pipe_m3.predict(X3te), float(cond3.y_train.mean())
    )
    R["elasticidades"] = mdl.cross_price_elasticities(cond2)
    R["diag_elasticidad"] = mdl.elasticity_diagnostics(cond2)
    valores = mdl.expected_value(
        proba, pipe_m2.predict_proba(X_test), pipe_m2.classes_, pipe_m3.predict(X_test),
        df, prep.X_test.index,
    )
    R["captura"] = mdl.value_capture(valores, X_test)

    log("· capa prescriptiva (P1/P2/P3)")
    bundle = px.fit_bundle(df, modelo_m0)
    supuesto = px.MarginAssumption()
    base = px.score_scenario(bundle, df, supuesto)
    cantidad = float(base["lambda"].mean())
    a = supuesto.costo_contacto(df, cantidad)
    d = supuesto.costo_promo_dia(df, cantidad)
    R["margen_base"] = float(base["margen_total"].sum())

    R["frontera"] = px.p1_frontier(
        base, a, fracciones=(0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50), fatiga=8
    )
    R["p1"] = px.solve_p1(base, a, int(0.20 * len(base)), fatiga=8)
    R["politicas"] = px.p1_vs_policies(base, X_test, a, int(0.20 * len(base)), fatiga=8)
    R["verif_p1"] = px.verify_p1_optimality(base, a, int(0.20 * len(base)), fatiga=8)

    valores_promo = px.promo_value_matrix(bundle, df, supuesto)
    calendario = px.observed_calendar(df)
    R["valor_promo"] = valores_promo
    R["p2"] = px.solve_p2(valores_promo, calendario, d, supuesto.tope_promo_por_marca)
    R["verif_p2"] = px.verify_p2_additivity(
        bundle, df, supuesto, R["p2"].attrs["calendario"], R["p2"].attrs["ganancia"], d
    )

    grilla = px.price_grid(df, supuesto, banda=0.10)
    R["respuesta_precio"] = px.price_response(bundle, df, supuesto, grilla)
    R["p3_sin_r7"] = px.solve_p3(R["respuesta_precio"], imponer_r7=False)
    R["p3_con_r7"] = px.solve_p3(R["respuesta_precio"], imponer_r7=True)
    R["verif_p3"] = px.verify_p3_plan(
        bundle, df, supuesto, R["p3_con_r7"].attrs["plan"],
        R["p3_con_r7"].attrs["ganancia_aditiva_total"],
    )
    R["curvas"] = {j: px.propensity_curve(bundle, df, supuesto, j) for j in (1, 3, 4, 5)}
    R["elasticidad_seg"] = px.elasticity_by_segment(bundle, df, supuesto, marca=5)
    R["sensibilidad"] = px.margin_sensitivity(bundle, df)
    R["kpis"] = px.kpi_table(
        R["politicas"], R["p1"], R["p2"], R["verif_p3"],
        float(R["contra_baseline"].attrs["lift_modelo_top10"]),
        float(R["contra_baseline"].attrs["lift_baseline_top10"]),
        R["margen_base"],
    )
    R["impacto"] = px.business_area_impact(
        R["p1"], R["p2"], R["elasticidad_seg"], R["p3_con_r7"], base
    )
    R["supuesto_tabla"] = supuesto.as_table(df, cantidad)
    R["margenes"] = supuesto.margenes(df)
    R["costos"] = {"contacto": a, "promo_dia": d, "cantidad_media": cantidad}
    log("· listo")
    return R


def _pr_curve(y, proba, puntos: int = 60) -> pd.DataFrame:
    from sklearn.metrics import precision_recall_curve

    p, r, _ = precision_recall_curve(y, proba)
    idx = np.linspace(0, len(p) - 1, puntos).astype(int)
    return pd.DataFrame({"recall": r[idx].round(4), "precision": p[idx].round(4)})


def _calibration(y, proba, bins: int = 10) -> pd.DataFrame:
    d = pd.DataFrame({"p": proba, "y": np.asarray(y)})
    d["bin"] = pd.qcut(d["p"], q=bins, duplicates="drop")
    g = d.groupby("bin", observed=True).agg(
        predicha=("p", "mean"), observada=("y", "mean"), n=("y", "size")
    )
    return g.reset_index(drop=True).round(4)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# dashboard/data.json
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def build_payload(R: dict) -> dict:
    """Shape the computed tables into what ``index.html`` consumes."""
    encuadre, segs, modelos, presc = (
        _frozen("encuadre.json"), _frozen("segmentos.json"),
        _frozen("modelos.json"), _frozen("prescriptivo.json"),
    )
    df = R["df"]
    p1, p2, p3 = R["p1"], R["p2"], R["p3_con_r7"]
    politicas = R["politicas"]
    # La ganancia de P1 se mide contra la **politica vigente** (ordenar por tasa
    # historica), que es lo que la empresa hace hoy. Compararla contra ordenar
    # por propension responde otra pregunta -- de diseno, no de negocio -- y
    # mezclar las dos daria dos cifras distintas en la misma pagina.
    ganancia_p1 = float(
        politicas[politicas["politica"].str.startswith("P1")]["objetivo_neto"].iloc[0]
        - politicas[politicas["politica"].str.startswith("politica historica")]["objetivo_neto"].iloc[0]
    )

    return {
        "meta": {
            "proyecto": "Modelo de Propension de Compra · Ferreycorp",
            "generado": pd.Timestamp.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
            "filas": int(len(df)),
            "columnas": int(df.shape[1]),
            "clientes": int(df[cfg.KEY_CLIENT].nunique()),
            "dias": int(df[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()),
            "split_day": cfg.SPLIT_DAY,
            "aviso": "La pagina no ejecuta los modelos en vivo: cada combinacion de los controles se calculo al generar data.js.",
            "huellas": {
                "encuadre_train": encuadre.get("split", {}).get("train", {}).get("fingerprint"),
                "encuadre_test": encuadre.get("split", {}).get("test", {}).get("fingerprint"),
                "segmentos": segs.get("fingerprint"),
                "modelos": modelos.get("fecha"),
                "prescriptivo": presc.get("fecha"),
            },
            "fases": [
                {"fase": "0 · Encuadre", "estado": "congelado", "detalle": "split dia 550 con huella SHA-256"},
                {"fase": "1 · EDA", "estado": "cerrado", "detalle": "20 figuras, 13/13 controles de tratamiento"},
                {"fase": "2 · Features", "estado": "cerrado", "detalle": "83 columnas, ajuste solo en train (E10)"},
                {"fase": "3 · Segmentacion M0", "estado": "congelado", "detalle": f"k=3, ARI 1.0, huella {str(segs.get('fingerprint'))[:8]}"},
                {"fase": "4 · M1/M2/M3", "estado": "cerrado", "detalle": f"PR-AUC {modelos.get('m1', {}).get('pr_auc_test')}"},
                {"fase": "5 · P1/P2/P3", "estado": "cerrado", "detalle": "optimalidad de P1 verificada contra el LP"},
                {"fase": "6 · Despliegue", "estado": "documentado", "detalle": "arquitectura multi-cloud + scoring ejecutable"},
            ],
        },
        "resumen": {
            "tasa_base": float(R["tasa"]["tasa_compra"]),
            "pr_auc": float(R["m1_test"]["pr_auc"]),
            "pr_auc_azar": float(R["m1_test"]["pr_auc_azar"]),
            "lift_top10": float(R["contra_baseline"].attrs["lift_modelo_top10"]),
            "lift_baseline": float(R["contra_baseline"].attrs["lift_baseline_top10"]),
            "margen_base": round(R["margen_base"], 2),
            "ganancia_p1": round(ganancia_p1, 2),
            "ganancia_p2": float(p2.attrs["ganancia"]),
            "ganancia_p3": float(R["verif_p3"].attrs["ganancia_real"]),
            "captura_top10": float(
                R["captura"][
                    (R["captura"]["presupuesto"] == "top 10%")
                    & (R["captura"]["politica"] == "valor esperado (M1xM2xM3)")
                ]["pct_del_ingreso"].iloc[0]
            ),
            "captura_historica_top10": float(
                R["captura"][
                    (R["captura"]["presupuesto"] == "top 10%")
                    & (R["captura"]["politica"] == "politica historica")
                ]["pct_del_ingreso"].iloc[0]
            ),
        },
        "eda": {
            "marcas": _records(
                R["brand_share"]
                .rename(columns={cfg.TARGET_BRAND: "marca"})
                .merge(R["promo_lift"][["marca", "frecuencia_promo", "lift", "respuesta_vs_inversion"]], on="marca")
                .merge(R["promo_incidencia"][["marca", "delta_pp", "z", "efecto"]], on="marca")
                .merge(R["ladder"][["marca", "n_puntos_precio", "precio_min", "precio_max"]], on="marca")
            ),
            "brecha_precio": _records(R["gap"]),
            "temporal": _records(R["temporal"]),
            "anomalias": _records(R["anomalias"]),
            "demografia": _records(R["demografia"]),
            "baseline": _records(R["baseline"]),
            "lecturas": {
                "temporal": R["temporal"].attrs["lectura"],
                "promo": R["promo_incidencia"].attrs["lectura"],
                "anomalias": R["anomalias"].attrs["lectura"],
                "brecha": R["gap"].attrs["lectura"],
            },
        },
        "segmentos": {
            "k": int(R["m0"].k),
            "silhouette": float(R["m0"].silhouette),
            "nombres": {str(k): v for k, v in R["m0"].nombres.items()},
            "perfil": _records(R["perfil_seg"]),
            "comportamiento": _records(R["comportamiento_seg"]),
            "marcas": _records(R["marcas_seg"]),
            "promo": _records(R["promo_seg"]),
            "seleccion_k": _records(R["seleccion_k"]),
            "pca": _records(R["pca"].reset_index()[["id", "pc1", "pc2", "segmento"]].round(3)),
            "lecturas": {
                "comportamiento": R["comportamiento_seg"].attrs["lectura"],
                "marcas": R["marcas_seg"].attrs["lectura"],
                "promo": R["promo_seg"].attrs["lectura"],
                "seleccion_k": R["seleccion_k"].attrs["lectura"],
            },
        },
        "modelos": {
            "m1": json.loads(R["m1_test"].to_json(double_precision=6)),
            "m2": json.loads(R["m2_test"].to_json(double_precision=6)),
            "m3": json.loads(R["m3_test"].to_json(double_precision=6)),
            "lift": _records(R["lift"]),
            "contra_baseline": _records(R["contra_baseline"]),
            "umbral": _records(R["umbral"]),
            "shap": _records(R["shap"]),
            "curva_pr": _records(R["curva_pr"]),
            "calibracion": _records(R["calibracion"]),
            "captura": _records(R["captura"]),
            "elasticidades": _records(R["elasticidades"].reset_index(names="share")),
            "diag_elasticidad": _records(R["diag_elasticidad"]),
            "lecturas": {
                "umbral": R["umbral"].attrs["lectura"],
                "shap": R["shap"].attrs["lectura"],
                "captura": R["captura"].attrs["lectura"],
                "elasticidades": R["elasticidades"].attrs["lectura"],
                "diag": R["diag_elasticidad"].attrs["lectura"],
                "contra_baseline": R["contra_baseline"].attrs["lectura"],
            },
        },
        "decisiones": {
            "supuestos": _records(R["supuesto_tabla"]),
            "margenes": _records(R["margenes"]),
            "costos": {k: round(float(v), 4) for k, v in R["costos"].items()},
            "p1": {
                "frontera": _records(R["frontera"]),
                "politicas": _records(R["politicas"]),
                "verificacion": _records(R["verif_p1"]),
                "contactos_utiles": int(R["frontera"].attrs["contactos_utiles"]),
                "pct_visitas_utiles": float(R["frontera"].attrs["pct_visitas_utiles"]),
                "marcas_ofrecidas": {str(k): int(v) for k, v in p1.attrs["marcas_ofrecidas"].items()},
                "lectura": politicas.attrs["lectura"],
            },
            "p2": {
                "reasignacion": _records(p2),
                "valor_observado": float(p2.attrs["valor_observado"]),
                "valor_optimo": float(p2.attrs["valor_optimo"]),
                "ganancia": float(p2.attrs["ganancia"]),
                "dias_presupuesto": int(p2.attrs["dias_gastados_observado"]),
                "error_aditivo": float(R["verif_p2"].attrs["error_relativo"]),
                "ganancia_verificada": float(R["verif_p2"].attrs["ganancia_real"]),
                "lectura": p2.attrs["lectura"],
            },
            "p3": {
                "sin_r7": _records(R["p3_sin_r7"]),
                "con_r7": _records(R["p3_con_r7"]),
                "costo_r7": round(
                    float(R["p3_sin_r7"].attrs["ganancia_aditiva_total"]
                          - R["p3_con_r7"].attrs["ganancia_aditiva_total"]), 2
                ),
                "respuesta": _records(R["respuesta_precio"]),
                "ganancia_verificada": float(R["verif_p3"].attrs["ganancia_real"]),
                "marcas_congeladas": list(px.MARCAS_SIN_ELASTICIDAD),
                "lectura": R["verif_p3"].attrs["lectura"],
            },
            "curvas": {
                str(j): {
                    "puntos": _records(c),
                    "identificada": bool(c.attrs["identificada"]),
                    "lectura": c.attrs["lectura"],
                }
                for j, c in R["curvas"].items()
            },
            "elasticidad_segmento": _records(R["elasticidad_seg"]),
            "sensibilidad": _records(R["sensibilidad"]),
            "kpis": _records(R["kpis"]),
            "impacto": _records(R["impacto"]),
            "lecturas": {
                "sensibilidad": R["sensibilidad"].attrs["lectura"],
                "kpis": R["kpis"].attrs["lectura"],
                "elasticidad_segmento": R["elasticidad_seg"].attrs["lectura"],
            },
        },
    }


def export_data(R: dict | None = None, path: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    """Write ``dashboard/data.json`` and its ``data.js`` twin.

    Both, on purpose. Opening ``index.html`` with a double click means the page
    runs on ``file://``, where ``fetch`` of a local JSON is blocked by CORS but
    a ``<script src>`` is not. So the page reads ``data.js``, and the ``.json``
    stays for anything else that wants the numbers.
    """
    R = R or compute()
    path = path or (DASHBOARD_DIR / "data.json")
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    payload = build_payload(R)
    crudo = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1, default=str)
    path.write_text(crudo, encoding="utf-8")
    (path.parent / "data.js").write_text(
        "// Generado por src/ferreyros/dashboard.py -- no editar a mano.\n"
        "window.FERREYROS_DATA = " + crudo + ";\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return path


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# docs/figuras/*.png -- shared by the documentation and the deck
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def export_figures(R: dict | None = None, directorio: Path | None = None) -> list[Path]:
    """Render the figures the PDF deliverables embed."""
    import matplotlib

    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    from . import plots

    R = R or compute()
    directorio = directorio or FIGURES_DIR
    directorio.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    plots.set_style()

    tareas = [
        ("eda_promo_incidencia", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_promo_incidence_by_brand(R["promo_incidencia"], ax=ax)),
        ("eda_promo_lift", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_promo_lift(R["promo_lift"], ax=ax)),
        ("eda_baseline", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_targeting_baseline(R["baseline"], ax=ax)),
        ("m0_segmentos_pca", (7.5, 5.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_pca_segments(R["pca"], ax=ax)),
        ("m0_marcas_por_segmento", (8.5, 3.6), lambda ax: plots.plot_segment_brand_share(R["marcas_seg"], ax=ax)),
        ("m1_pr", (7.5, 4.8), lambda ax: plots.plot_pr_roc(R["y_test"], R["proba_m1"], ax=ax)),
        ("m1_umbral", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_threshold_value(R["y_test"], R["proba_m1"], ax=ax)),
        ("m1_shap", (8, 5), lambda ax: plots.plot_shap_global(R["shap"], ax=ax)),
        ("m1_captura", (8.5, 4.5), lambda ax: plots.plot_value_capture(R["captura"], ax=ax)),
        ("m2_elasticidades", (8, 4), lambda ax: plots.plot_elasticities(R["elasticidades"], ax=ax)),
        ("p1_frontera", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_p1_frontier(R["frontera"], ax=ax)),
        ("p2_reasignacion", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_promo_reallocation(R["p2"], ax=ax)),
        ("p3_respuesta_precio", (9, 4.2), lambda ax: plots.plot_price_response(R["respuesta_precio"], ax=ax)),
        ("p5_curvas_propension", (8.5, 4.5), lambda ax: plots.plot_propensity_curves(R["curvas"], ax=ax)),
        ("p5_waterfall", (8, 4.5), lambda ax: plots.plot_value_waterfall(
            R["margen_base"],
            {
                "P1 targeting": round(float(
                    R["politicas"][R["politicas"]["politica"].str.startswith("P1")]["objetivo_neto"].iloc[0]
                    - R["politicas"][R["politicas"]["politica"].str.startswith("politica historica")]["objetivo_neto"].iloc[0]
                ), 2),
                "P2 calendario": float(R["p2"].attrs["ganancia"]),
                "P3 pricing": float(R["verif_p3"].attrs["ganancia_real"]),
            },
            ax=ax,
        )),
    ]

    escritas = []
    for nombre, tamanio, dibuja in tareas:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=tamanio)
        dibuja(ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        ruta = directorio / f"{nombre}.png"
        fig.savefig(ruta, dpi=130, bbox_inches="tight")
        plt.close(fig)
        escritas.append(ruta)
    return escritas


def export_all(verbose: bool = True) -> dict:
    """One pass: compute, write ``data.json`` and render the figures."""
    R = compute(verbose=verbose)
    datos = export_data(R)
    figuras = export_figures(R)
    if verbose:
        print(f"· {datos.relative_to(cfg.PROJECT_ROOT)} ({datos.stat().st_size / 1024:.0f} KB)")
        print(f"· {len(figuras)} figuras en {FIGURES_DIR.relative_to(cfg.PROJECT_ROOT)}")
    return {"data": datos, "figuras": figuras}

"""Ferreyros -- purchase-propensity solution for the Ferreycorp technical test.

Layered on the documents in ``docs/``:

* ``brief-problema.md``        -- the optimization problem and the ML layer that feeds it
* ``brief-alineamiento-eda.md`` -- the matrix this package's functions satisfy
* ``brief-metodologia.md``     -- the end-to-end methodology

Phase 0 (framing) freezes the analysis contract in ``artifacts/encuadre.json``;
later phases call ``framing.verify_frozen(df)`` before doing anything else.

Typical notebook entry point::

    from ferreyros import config as cfg, data, features, plots, preprocess
    from ferreyros.audit import control_cell, treatment_audit
    from ferreyros.eda import objective, decisions, constraints, segments

    df = data.load_raw()
    control_cell(df)
    framing.freeze(df)                # Fase 0: congela el contrato de analisis
    prepared = preprocess.prepare()   # matrices de modelado, fit solo en train
"""

from . import (
    audit,
    config,
    dashboard,
    data,
    eda,
    features,
    framing,
    models,
    plots,
    prescriptive,
    preprocess,
    segmentation,
)
from .data import add_paid_price_and_spend, load_raw, purchases_only, temporal_split
from .preprocess import prepare

__all__ = [
    "config",
    "dashboard",
    "data",
    "eda",
    "features",
    "framing",
    "preprocess",
    "segmentation",
    "models",
    "prescriptive",
    "plots",
    "audit",
    "load_raw",
    "purchases_only",
    "add_paid_price_and_spend",
    "temporal_split",
    "prepare",
]


def main() -> None:
    """Smoke entry point: load the data and print the control cell verdict."""
    df = load_raw()
    report = audit.control_cell(df)
    ok = report.attrs["all_ok"]
    print(f"Celda de control: {'OK' if ok else str(report.attrs['n_fallas']) + ' discrepancias'}")
    if not ok:
        print(report[report["veredicto"] != "OK"].to_string(index=False))

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Concatenate the whole solution into ``entregables/codigo_solucion.txt``.

The brief accepts Word or TXT. TXT, because it needs no library to produce, no
library to open, and it diffs.

    uv run python scripts/export_codigo.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SALIDA = ROOT / "entregables" / "codigo_solucion.txt"

ORDEN = [
    ("Configuracion y contrato de datos", ["src/ferreyros/config.py", "src/ferreyros/data.py"]),
    ("Fase 0 · Encuadre congelado", ["src/ferreyros/framing.py"]),
    ("Fase 1 · EDA", [
        "src/ferreyros/eda/__init__.py", "src/ferreyros/eda/objective.py",
        "src/ferreyros/eda/decisions.py", "src/ferreyros/eda/constraints.py",
        "src/ferreyros/eda/segments.py", "src/ferreyros/eda/dynamics.py",
        "src/ferreyros/eda/anomalies.py", "src/ferreyros/audit.py",
    ]),
    ("Fase 2 · Features y preprocesamiento", [
        "src/ferreyros/features.py", "src/ferreyros/preprocess.py",
    ]),
    ("Fase 3 · Segmentacion (M0)", ["src/ferreyros/segmentation.py"]),
    ("Fase 4 · Modelos M1, M2, M3", ["src/ferreyros/models.py"]),
    ("Fase 5 · Capa prescriptiva (P1, P2, P3)", ["src/ferreyros/prescriptive.py"]),
    ("Fase 6 · Despliegue y scoring", [
        "scripts/score_batch.py", "scripts/serve_api.py",
    ]),
    ("Figuras, dashboard y paquete", [
        "src/ferreyros/plots.py", "src/ferreyros/dashboard.py", "src/ferreyros/__init__.py",
        "scripts/export_codigo.py", "scripts/build_entregables.py",
    ]),
]

NOTEBOOKS = [
    "notebooks/00_encuadre.ipynb", "notebooks/01_eda.ipynb", "notebooks/02_procesamiento.ipynb",
    "notebooks/03_segmentacion.ipynb", "notebooks/04_modelos.ipynb", "notebooks/05_pricing.ipynb",
]


def barra(txt: str, char: str = "=") -> str:
    return f"{char * 78}\n{txt}\n{char * 78}\n"


def main() -> None:
    SALIDA.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    partes: list[str] = []
    indice: list[str] = []

    partes.append(barra("PRUEBA TECNICA DATA SCIENTIST · FERREYCORP\nMODELO DE PROPENSION DE COMPRA — CODIGO DE LA SOLUCION"))
    partes.append(
        f"Generado: {date.today().isoformat()}\n"
        "Repositorio: estructura completa en el ZIP / GitHub adjunto\n\n"
        "El codigo esta organizado por fases. La logica vive en modulos de 'src/ferreyros/';\n"
        "los notebooks solo orquestan, muestran y concluyen. Esa separacion es deliberada:\n"
        "un notebook que contiene la logica no se puede probar ni reutilizar en produccion.\n\n"
        "Para ejecutar todo de principio a fin:\n"
        "    uv sync\n"
        "    uv run jupyter nbconvert --to notebook --execute --inplace notebooks/*.ipynb\n"
        "    uv run python scripts/score_batch.py\n"
    )

    cuerpo: list[str] = []
    for seccion, rutas in ORDEN:
        cuerpo.append("\n" + barra(seccion))
        for ruta in rutas:
            f = ROOT / ruta
            if not f.exists():
                continue
            texto = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            lineas = texto.count("\n") + 1
            indice.append(f"  {ruta:<42} {lineas:>5} lineas")
            cuerpo.append(barra(f"ARCHIVO: {ruta}  ({lineas} lineas)", "-"))
            cuerpo.append(texto.rstrip() + "\n")

    cuerpo.append("\n" + barra("NOTEBOOKS · codigo de las celdas ejecutadas"))
    for ruta in NOTEBOOKS:
        f = ROOT / ruta
        if not f.exists():
            continue
        nb = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        celdas = [c for c in nb["cells"] if c["cell_type"] == "code"]
        indice.append(f"  {ruta:<42} {len(celdas):>5} celdas de codigo")
        cuerpo.append(barra(f"NOTEBOOK: {ruta}  ({len(celdas)} celdas de codigo)", "-"))
        for i, c in enumerate(celdas, 1):
            cuerpo.append(f"# --- celda {i} ---\n{''.join(c['source']).rstrip()}\n")

    partes.append("\n" + barra("INDICE", "-"))
    partes.append("\n".join(indice) + "\n")
    partes.extend(cuerpo)

    SALIDA.write_text("\n".join(partes), encoding="utf-8")
    kb = SALIDA.stat().st_size / 1024
    print(f"escrito {SALIDA.relative_to(ROOT)} · {kb:.0f} KB · {len(indice)} archivos")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# --- celda 1 ---
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path.cwd().parent if Path.cwd().name == "notebooks" else Path.cwd()
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import pandas as pd

from ferreyros import config as cfg, data, framing

pd.set_option("display.max_columns", 40, "display.width", 200, "display.max_colwidth", 95)

df = data.load_raw()
contrato = framing.FramingContract()
print(f"dataset: {df.shape[0]:,} filas x {df.shape[1]} columnas")

# --- celda 2 ---
unidad = framing.verify_unit_of_analysis(df, contrato)
display(unidad)
assert unidad.attrs["unidad_valida"], "La unidad de análisis no está bien definida"

# --- celda 3 ---
framing.target_definition(df)

# --- celda 4 ---
adm = framing.admissibility_table(df)
display(adm)
print(f"admisibles: {adm.attrs['n_admisibles']}  |  "
      f"excluidas: {adm.attrs['n_excluidas']}  |  "
      f"sin clasificar: {adm.attrs['sin_clasificar']}")
assert adm.attrs["sin_clasificar"] == 0, "Hay columnas sin clasificar por el contrato"

# --- celda 5 ---
# Lo que nunca puede aparecer en la matriz de M1, congelado como conjunto
sorted(framing.forbidden_columns())

# --- celda 6 ---
framing.validation_plan(contrato)

# --- celda 7 ---
split = framing.split_manifest_table(df, contrato)
display(split)
print("split balanceado:", split.attrs["balanceado"])

# --- celda 8 ---
reporte = framing.phase0_report(df, contrato)
display(reporte)
assert reporte.attrs["fase0_completa"], "La Fase 0 tiene decisiones sin cerrar"

# --- celda 9 ---
payload = framing.freeze(df, contrato)
print(f"contrato escrito en: {framing.FRAMING_PATH.relative_to(ROOT)}")
print()
print(json.dumps(payload["split"], indent=2))

# --- celda 10 ---
display(contrato.as_table())

# --- celda 11 ---
ver = framing.verify_frozen(df)
display(ver)
assert ver.attrs["coincide"], "El dataset ya no coincide con el encuadre congelado"

# --- celda 12 ---
from ferreyros import preprocess

prepared = preprocess.prepare()
display(prepared.shapes)

# --- celda 13 ---
obedece = framing.verify_prepared_against_contract(prepared)
display(obedece)
assert obedece.attrs["obedece_contrato"], "Las matrices de modelado no respetan el encuadre"
print("Las matrices de modelado respetan el encuadre congelado.")

for forbidden in [cfg.TARGET_BRAND, cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, *cfg.EXCLUDED_COLS, "gasto", "precio_pagado"]:

NOTEBOOK: notebooks/02_procesamiento.ipynb  (14 celdas de codigo)

# --- celda 1 ---
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Permite ejecutar el notebook sin instalar el paquete (uv run jupyter / VS Code).
ROOT = Path.cwd().parent if Path.cwd().name == "notebooks" else Path.cwd()
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import pandas as pd

from ferreyros import audit, config as cfg, data, features
from ferreyros import preprocess as prep

pd.set_option("display.max_columns", 60, "display.width", 180)
pd.set_option("display.max_colwidth", 90)

print("raíz del proyecto:", ROOT)

# --- celda 2 ---
df_raw = data.load_raw()
control = audit.control_cell(df_raw)

print(f"Cifras verificadas: {len(control)}  |  discrepancias: {control.attrs['n_fallas']}")
assert control.attrs["all_ok"], "La celda de control no cuadra: revisar la carga antes de seguir"
control.head(8)

# --- celda 3 ---
# `precio_pagado` y `gasto` (control A7). Son resultados de la visita: sirven para construir
# el ticket histórico, pero nunca entran como features de M1.
df = data.add_paid_price_and_spend(df_raw)
print("columnas:", df_raw.shape[1], "→", df.shape[1])

# --- celda 4 ---
# El orden importa: se parte ANTES de ajustar nada.
train_raw, test_raw = data.temporal_split(df)
print(f"train: días {train_raw[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()}–{train_raw[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()}  "
      f"({len(train_raw):,} filas)")
print(f"test:  días {test_raw[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()}–{test_raw[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()}  "
      f"({len(test_raw):,} filas)")

reference = features.fit_reference(train_raw)
reference.describe()

# --- celda 5 ---
# ¿El límite del ajuste es material o cosmético? Comparamos cada estadístico contra el valor
# que tendría ajustado sobre train+test.
ref_check = features.verify_reference_fit(
    features.build_features(df, reference=reference)
)
display(ref_check)
print(ref_check.attrs["lectura"])
assert ref_check.attrs["e10_features_ok"], "Los estadísticos de referencia no son solo de train"

# --- celda 6 ---
feat = features.build_features(df, reference=reference)
print("columnas:", df.shape[1], "→", feat.shape[1],
      f"({len(features.all_feature_names())} variables derivadas)")
features.feature_summary(feat)

# --- celda 7 ---
leak = features.verify_no_leakage(feat)
display(leak)
assert leak.attrs["sin_fuga"], "Fuga detectada en las features de historial"
print("Sin fuga: ninguna feature de historial contiene información de la visita actual.")

# --- celda 8 ---
X_train = features.feature_matrix(feat[feat[cfg.KEY_TIME] <= cfg.SPLIT_DAY])
X_test = features.feature_matrix(feat[feat[cfg.KEY_TIME] > cfg.SPLIT_DAY])[list(X_train.columns)]

print("matriz M1:", X_train.shape[1], "features admisibles")
for forbidden in [cfg.TARGET_BRAND, cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, *cfg.EXCLUDED_COLS,
                  "precio_pagado", "gasto"]:
    assert forbidden not in X_train.columns, f"{forbidden} no puede ser feature de M1"
    print(f"  excluida: {forbidden}")

# --- celda 9 ---
prep.column_roles(X_train)

# --- celda 10 ---
prepared = prep.prepare(df)
prepared.shapes

# --- celda 11 ---
# La narrativa de arriba y la función de una línea deben coincidir exactamente.
assert list(prepared.X_train.columns) == list(X_train.columns)
assert prepared.X_train.equals(X_train) and prepared.X_test.equals(X_test)
print("La matriz construida paso a paso y la de prepare() son idénticas.")

prep.preprocessing_summary(prepared).to_frame("valor")

# --- celda 12 ---
e10 = prep.verify_train_only_fit(prepared)
display(e10)
print(e10.attrs["lectura"])
assert e10.attrs["e10_ok"], "El preprocesamiento aprendió algo de test"

# --- celda 13 ---
oor = prep.out_of_range_report(prepared)
display(oor)
print("features que extrapolan:", oor.attrs["n_features_extrapolan"], "de", X_train.shape[1])

# --- celda 14 ---
aud = audit.treatment_audit(
    df_raw,
    feature_cols=list(prepared.X_train.columns),
    featured_df=feat,
    prepared=prepared,            # cierra E10
    notebook_runs_clean=True,     # este notebook corre de extremo a extremo
)
display(aud)
print("bloqueantes fallados:", aud.attrs["bloqueantes_fallados"],
      "| pendientes:", aud.attrs["pendientes"])
assert aud.attrs["bloqueantes_fallados"] == 0, "Hay controles bloqueantes en FALLA"
assert aud.attrs["pendientes"] == 0, "Quedan controles pendientes"

NOTEBOOK: notebooks/03_segmentacion.ipynb  (20 celdas de codigo)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# --- celda 1 ---
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path.cwd().parent if Path.cwd().name == "notebooks" else Path.cwd()
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd

from ferreyros import config as cfg, data, framing, plots, preprocess
from ferreyros import segmentation as m0

plots.set_style()
pd.set_option("display.max_columns", 40, "display.width", 200)

df = data.add_paid_price_and_spend(data.load_raw())
print(f"dataset: {df.shape[0]:,} filas x {df.shape[1]} columnas")

# --- celda 2 ---
ver_encuadre = framing.verify_frozen(df)
display(ver_encuadre)
assert ver_encuadre.attrs["coincide"], "El dataset ya no coincide con el encuadre congelado"

# --- celda 3 ---
M = m0.client_matrix(df)
display(M.head())
print("matriz cruda:", M.shape)

# --- celda 4 ---
diseno = m0.verify_design(df)
display(diseno)
print(diseno.attrs["lectura"])
assert diseno.attrs["apto"], "La matriz de M0 no es admisible"

# --- celda 5 ---
seleccion = m0.select_k(df)
display(seleccion)
print(seleccion.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_k_selection(seleccion, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 6 ---
escaneo = m0.stability_scan(df, ks=(2, 3, 4, 9), n_repeats=20)
display(escaneo)
print(escaneo.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 7 ---
estabilidad = m0.stability(df, k=3, n_repeats=20)
display(estabilidad.head())
print(estabilidad.attrs["lectura"])
assert estabilidad.attrs["estable"], "La partición elegida no es reproducible"

# --- celda 8 ---
sensibilidad = m0.encoding_sensitivity(df, k=3)
display(sensibilidad)
print(sensibilidad.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 9 ---
modelo = m0.fit(df, k=3)
print(f"k={modelo.k}  |  semilla={modelo.random_state}  |  silhouette={modelo.silhouette}")
print(f"columnas: {list(modelo.columnas)}")
display(modelo.tabla_nombres)

# --- celda 10 ---
perfil = m0.segment_profile(modelo, df)
display(perfil)
print(perfil.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 11 ---
proyeccion = m0.pca_projection(modelo, df)
print(proyeccion.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 5.5))
plots.plot_pca_segments(proyeccion, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 12 ---
m0.pca_loadings(proyeccion)

# --- celda 13 ---
comportamiento = m0.segment_behaviour(modelo, df)
display(comportamiento)
print(comportamiento.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_segment_behaviour(comportamiento, float(df[cfg.TARGET_INCIDENCE].mean()), ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 14 ---
marcas = m0.segment_brand_share(modelo, df)
display(marcas)
print(marcas.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.5, 3.6))
plots.plot_segment_brand_share(marcas, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 15 ---
respuesta = m0.segment_promo_response(modelo, df)
display(respuesta)
print(respuesta.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_segment_promo_response(respuesta, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 16 ---
sin_segmento = preprocess.prepare()
con_segmento = m0.prepare_with_segment(modelo, df)

print("sin segmento:", sin_segmento.X_train.shape, "→", len(sin_segmento.feature_names), "columnas de salida")
print("con segmento:", con_segmento.X_train.shape, "→", len(con_segmento.feature_names), "columnas de salida")
print("columnas nuevas:", [n for n in con_segmento.feature_names if n not in sin_segmento.feature_names])

# --- celda 17 ---
# El encuadre de la Fase 0 y el control E10 de la Fase 2 siguen cumpliéndose con la feature nueva
obedece = framing.verify_prepared_against_contract(con_segmento)
display(obedece)
assert obedece.attrs["obedece_contrato"], "La matriz con segmento no respeta el encuadre"

e10 = preprocess.verify_train_only_fit(con_segmento)
assert e10.attrs["e10_ok"], "El ajuste dejó de ser solo sobre train"
print(e10.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 18 ---
payload = m0.freeze(modelo, df, estabilidad)
print("congelado en:", m0.SEGMENTS_PATH.relative_to(ROOT))
print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in payload.items() if k != "asignacion"}, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

# --- celda 19 ---
ver = m0.verify_frozen(modelo)
display(ver)
print(ver.attrs["lectura"])
assert ver.attrs["coincide"], "El modelo ajustado no coincide con la segmentación congelada"

# --- celda 20 ---
reporte = m0.phase3_report(modelo, df, seleccion, estabilidad)
display(reporte)
print(reporte.attrs["lectura"])
assert reporte.attrs["fase3_completa"], "La Fase 3 tiene decisiones sin cerrar"

NOTEBOOK: notebooks/04_modelos.ipynb  (26 celdas de codigo)

# --- celda 1 ---
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path.cwd().parent if Path.cwd().name == "notebooks" else Path.cwd()
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ferreyros import config as cfg, data, framing, models as mdl, plots, preprocess
from ferreyros import segmentation as m0

plots.set_style()
pd.set_option("display.max_columns", 40, "display.width", 200)

t_inicio = time.time()
df = data.add_paid_price_and_spend(data.load_raw())
print(f"dataset: {df.shape[0]:,} filas x {df.shape[1]} columnas")

# --- celda 2 ---
ver_encuadre = framing.verify_frozen(df)
assert ver_encuadre.attrs["coincide"], "El dataset ya no coincide con el encuadre congelado"
print("encuadre (Fase 0): las", len(ver_encuadre), "verificaciones del contrato coinciden")

modelo_m0 = m0.fit(df, k=3)
ver_segmentos = m0.verify_frozen(modelo_m0)
assert ver_segmentos.attrs["coincide"], "La segmentación no coincide con la congelada"
print("segmentos (Fase 3):", ver_segmentos.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 3 ---
dfs = m0.attach_segment(df, modelo_m0)
prep = m0.prepare_with_segment(modelo_m0, df)
dias_train, dias_test = mdl.day_index(prep, df)

cond_m2 = mdl.prepare_conditional(cfg.TARGET_BRAND, df=dfs, extra=["segmento"])
cond_m3 = mdl.prepare_conditional(cfg.TARGET_QUANTITY, df=dfs, extra=["segmento"])

print("M1 (todas las visitas)")
display(prep.shapes)
print("\nM2 / M3 (solo compras)")
display(cond_m2.shapes)

# --- celda 4 ---
folds_tiempo = mdl.expanding_day_folds(dias_train)
display(mdl.fold_description(dias_train, folds_tiempo))

folds_estricto = mdl.grouped_time_folds(dias_train, prep.groups_train)
display(mdl.fold_description(dias_train, folds_estricto))

# --- celda 5 ---
t0 = time.time()
comparacion = mdl.cv_compare(prep.X_train, prep.y_train, dias_train, prep.groups_train)
display(comparacion)
display(comparacion.attrs["comparacion_pareada"])
print(comparacion.attrs["lectura"])
print(f"\n[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

# --- celda 6 ---
experimento = mdl.trend_feature_experiment(
    prep.X_train, prep.y_train, prep.X_test, prep.y_test, dias_train,
    mdl.m1_candidates()["1 · logistica"],
)
display(experimento)
print(experimento.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 7 ---
# Decisión tomada con la métrica en la mano: fuera.
X_train, X_test = mdl.drop_features(prep.X_train, prep.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)
X2_train, X2_test = mdl.drop_features(cond_m2.X_train, cond_m2.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)
X3_train, X3_test = mdl.drop_features(cond_m3.X_train, cond_m3.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)
print("columnas de M1:", prep.X_train.shape[1], "→", X_train.shape[1])

# --- celda 8 ---
rejilla = [{"C": c} for c in (0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0)]
tuning = mdl.tune(X_train, prep.y_train, dias_train, mdl.m1_candidates()["1 · logistica"], rejilla)
display(tuning[["configuracion", "pr_auc_media", "pr_auc_std", "roc_auc_media"]])
print(tuning.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 9 ---
mejor_C = tuning.attrs["mejores_params"]
pipe_m1, proba_m1 = mdl.fit_final(
    mdl.m1_candidates()["1 · logistica"], X_train, prep.y_train, X_test, mejor_C
)
m1_test = mdl.evaluate_binary(prep.y_test, proba_m1, f"logistica C={mejor_C['C']}")
display(m1_test.to_frame("valor"))

# --- celda 10 ---
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 5))
plots.plot_pr_roc(prep.y_test, proba_m1, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 11 ---
fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_calibration(prep.y_test, proba_m1, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 12 ---
lift = mdl.lift_table(prep.y_test, proba_m1)
display(lift)
print(lift.attrs["lectura"])

contra_baseline = mdl.baseline_policy_lift(X_test, prep.y_test, proba_m1)
display(contra_baseline)
print(contra_baseline.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_gains(contra_baseline, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 13 ---
umbral = mdl.threshold_by_cost(prep.y_test, proba_m1)
display(umbral)
print(umbral.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 14 ---
regla = mdl.verify_threshold_rule(prep.y_test, proba_m1)
display(regla)
print(regla.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_threshold_value(prep.y_test, proba_m1, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 15 ---
shap_g = mdl.shap_global(pipe_m1, X_test, top=12)
display(shap_g)
print(shap_g.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))
plots.plot_shap_global(shap_g, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 16 ---
local = mdl.shap_local(pipe_m1, X_test, posicion=0, top=8)
display(local)
print(local.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 17 ---
odds = mdl.odds_ratios(pipe_m1, top=10)
display(odds)
print(odds.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 18 ---
t0 = time.time()
comparacion_m2 = mdl.cv_compare_multiclass(
    X2_train, cond_m2.y_train, cond_m2.dias_train, cond_m2.groups_train
)
display(comparacion_m2)
print(comparacion_m2.attrs["lectura"])
print(f"\n[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

# --- celda 19 ---
pipe_m2 = mdl.pipeline(mdl.m2_candidates()["2 · random forest"], X2_train).fit(
    X2_train, cond_m2.y_train
)
proba_m2 = pipe_m2.predict_proba(X2_test)
m2_test = mdl.evaluate_multiclass(cond_m2.y_test, proba_m2, pipe_m2.classes_)
display(m2_test.to_frame("valor"))

display(mdl.brand_confusion(cond_m2.y_test, pipe_m2.predict(X2_test)))

# --- celda 20 ---
elasticidades = mdl.cross_price_elasticities(cond_m2)
display(elasticidades)
print(elasticidades.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4))
plots.plot_elasticities(elasticidades, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 21 ---
diagnostico = mdl.elasticity_diagnostics(cond_m2)
display(diagnostico)
print(diagnostico.attrs["lectura"])

variacion = mdl.price_variation_diagnostics(df, cond_m2)
display(variacion)
print(variacion.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 22 ---
t0 = time.time()
comparacion_m3 = mdl.cv_compare_count(
    X3_train, cond_m3.y_train, cond_m3.dias_train, cond_m3.groups_train
)
display(comparacion_m3)
print(comparacion_m3.attrs["lectura"])

pipe_m3 = mdl.pipeline(mdl.m3_candidates()["1 · poisson"], X3_train).fit(X3_train, cond_m3.y_train)
m3_test = mdl.evaluate_count(cond_m3.y_test, pipe_m3.predict(X3_test), float(cond_m3.y_train.mean()))
display(m3_test.to_frame("valor"))
print(f"[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

# --- celda 23 ---
proba_m2_full = pipe_m2.predict_proba(X_test)
lambda_full = pipe_m3.predict(X_test)

valores = mdl.expected_value(
    proba_m1, proba_m2_full, pipe_m2.classes_, lambda_full, df, prep.X_test.index
)
display(valores.head())
print(valores.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 24 ---
captura = mdl.value_capture(valores, X_test)
display(captura)
print(captura.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.5, 4.5))
plots.plot_value_capture(captura, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 25 ---
resultados = {
    "m1": {
        "modelo": f"logistica C={mejor_C['C']}",
        "features": int(X_train.shape[1]),
        "pr_auc_test": float(m1_test["pr_auc"]),
        "roc_auc_test": float(m1_test["roc_auc"]),
        "brier": float(m1_test["brier"]),
        "lift_top10": float(contra_baseline.attrs["lift_modelo_top10"]),
        "lift_top10_politica_historica": float(contra_baseline.attrs["lift_baseline_top10"]),
        "pr_auc_cv_estricta": float(
            comparacion[
                (comparacion["modelo"] == "1 · logistica")
                & comparacion["esquema"].str.startswith("cliente nuevo")
            ]["pr_auc_media"].iloc[0]
        ),
    },
    "m2": {
        "modelo_prediccion": "random forest",
        "modelo_elasticidades": "logit multinomial sin penalizacion",
        "log_loss_test": float(m2_test["log_loss"]),
        "accuracy_test": float(m2_test["accuracy"]),
        "elasticidades_propias": elasticidades.attrs["elasticidades_propias"],
        "marcas_identificadas": diagnostico.attrs["marcas_identificadas"],
        "marcas_no_identificadas": diagnostico.attrs["marcas_no_identificadas"],
    },
    "m3": {
        "modelo": "poisson",
        "mae_test": float(m3_test["mae"]),
        "rmse_test": float(m3_test["rmse"]),
        "mae_baseline": float(m3_test["mae_baseline_media"]),
    },
    "umbral": {
        "regla": "umbral = costo de contacto / margen por compra",
        "desvio_maximo_verificado": float(regla.attrs["max_desvio"]),
        "contactos_en_0.5_pct": float(umbral.attrs["pct_en_0.5"]),
    },
    "captura_de_valor_top10_pct": {
        fila["politica"]: float(fila["pct_del_ingreso"])
        for _, fila in captura[captura["presupuesto"] == "top 10%"].iterrows()
    },
    "features_descartadas": mdl.TREND_COLS,
}
payload = mdl.freeze(resultados)
print("congelado en:", mdl.MODELS_PATH.relative_to(ROOT))
print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)[:1200], "...")

# --- celda 26 ---
reporte = mdl.phase4_report(
    m1_test, lift, contra_baseline, umbral, regla, m2_test, diagnostico, m3_test, captura
)
display(reporte)
print(reporte.attrs["lectura"])
assert reporte.attrs["fase4_completa"], "La Fase 4 tiene requisitos sin cerrar"
print(f"\nnotebook completo en {time.time() - t_inicio:.0f}s")

NOTEBOOK: notebooks/05_pricing.ipynb  (23 celdas de codigo)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# --- celda 1 ---
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path.cwd().parent if Path.cwd().name == "notebooks" else Path.cwd()
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from ferreyros import config as cfg, data, framing, models as mdl, plots
from ferreyros import prescriptive as px, segmentation as m0

plots.set_style()
pd.set_option("display.max_columns", 40, "display.width", 200)

t_inicio = time.time()
df = data.add_paid_price_and_spend(data.load_raw())
print(f"dataset: {df.shape[0]:,} filas x {df.shape[1]} columnas")

# --- celda 2 ---
ver_encuadre = framing.verify_frozen(df)
assert ver_encuadre.attrs["coincide"], "El dataset ya no coincide con el encuadre congelado"

modelo_m0 = m0.fit(df, k=3)
ver_segmentos = m0.verify_frozen(modelo_m0)
assert ver_segmentos.attrs["coincide"], "La segmentación no coincide con la congelada"

metricas_m4 = json.loads((ROOT / "artifacts" / "modelos.json").read_text())
print("Fase 0 · encuadre:", "OK")
print("Fase 3 · segmentos:", ver_segmentos.attrs["lectura"])
print("Fase 4 · M1 PR-AUC {} | M2 accuracy {} | M3 MAE {}".format(
    metricas_m4["m1"]["pr_auc_test"], metricas_m4["m2"]["accuracy_test"], metricas_m4["m3"]["mae_test"]))
print("Fase 4 · marcas sin elasticidad identificada:", metricas_m4["m2"]["marcas_no_identificadas"])

# --- celda 3 ---
bundle = px.fit_bundle(df, modelo_m0)
display(bundle.resumen.to_frame("modelo"))

supuesto = px.MarginAssumption()
base = px.score_scenario(bundle, df, supuesto)
cantidad_media = float(base["lambda"].mean())
margen_base = float(base["margen_total"].sum())
print(f"horizonte: {len(base):,} visitas (dias {base[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()}-{base[cfg.KEY_TIME].max()})")
print(f"margen esperado del horizonte: {margen_base:.2f}  |  ingreso real observado: {base['ingreso_real'].sum():.2f}")

# --- celda 4 ---
display(supuesto.margenes(df))
display(supuesto.as_table(df, cantidad_media))
print(supuesto.as_table(df, cantidad_media).attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 5 ---
t0 = time.time()
curvas = {j: px.propensity_curve(bundle, df, supuesto, j) for j in (1, 3, 4, 5)}
for j, curva in curvas.items():
    print(f"— {curva.attrs['lectura']}\n")
print(f"[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

# --- celda 6 ---
display(curvas[5])

fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.5, 4.5))
plots.plot_propensity_curves(curvas, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 7 ---
elasticidad_seg = px.elasticity_by_segment(bundle, df, supuesto, marca=5)
display(elasticidad_seg)
print(elasticidad_seg.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 8 ---
a = supuesto.costo_contacto(df, cantidad_media)
d = supuesto.costo_promo_dia(df, cantidad_media)
presupuesto_contactos = int(0.20 * len(base))
FATIGA = 8

p1 = px.solve_p1(base, a, presupuesto_contactos, fatiga=FATIGA)
print(p1.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 9 ---
verificacion_p1 = px.verify_p1_optimality(base, a, presupuesto_contactos, fatiga=FATIGA)
display(verificacion_p1)
print(verificacion_p1.attrs["lectura"])
assert verificacion_p1.attrs["optimo"], "El greedy no alcanza el optimo del LP"

# --- celda 10 ---
# La politica vigente es ordenar por tasa de compra historica, asi que hay que
# traer esa columna de la matriz de features; usar pi en su lugar compararia el
# modelo contra si mismo y etiquetaria mal la fila.
prep = m0.prepare_with_segment(modelo_m0, df)
X_test = mdl.drop_features(prep.X_train, prep.X_test, mdl.TREND_COLS)[1]
assert X_test.index.equals(base.index), "El horizonte de P1 y la matriz de test no coinciden"

politicas = px.p1_vs_policies(base, X_test, a, presupuesto_contactos, fatiga=FATIGA)
display(politicas)
print(politicas.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 11 ---
frontera = px.p1_frontier(base, a, fatiga=FATIGA)
display(frontera)
print(frontera.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_p1_frontier(frontera, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 12 ---
t0 = time.time()
valores_promo = px.promo_value_matrix(bundle, df, supuesto)
display(valores_promo.head())
print(valores_promo.attrs["lectura"])
print(f"[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

# --- celda 13 ---
calendario_obs = px.observed_calendar(df)
print("dias-promo observados en el horizonte:", calendario_obs.sum().to_dict())
print("total:", int(calendario_obs.to_numpy().sum()), "dias-marca")

p2 = px.solve_p2(valores_promo, calendario_obs, d, supuesto.tope_promo_por_marca)
display(p2)
print(p2.attrs["lectura"])

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_promo_reallocation(p2, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 14 ---
verificacion_p2 = px.verify_p2_additivity(
    bundle, df, supuesto, p2.attrs["calendario"], p2.attrs["ganancia"], d
)
display(verificacion_p2)
print(verificacion_p2.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 15 ---
grilla = px.price_grid(df, supuesto, banda=0.10)
print("grilla acotada (+-10%):")
for j, puntos in grilla.items():
    congelada = " [CONGELADA: elasticidad no identificada]" if len(puntos) == 1 else ""
    print(f"  marca {j}: {puntos}{congelada}")

t0 = time.time()
respuesta = px.price_response(bundle, df, supuesto, grilla)
print(f"\n{respuesta.attrs['lectura']}")
print(f"[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

fig, ax = plt.subplots()
plots.plot_price_response(respuesta, ax=ax)
plt.show()

# --- celda 16 ---
p3_sin_r7 = px.solve_p3(respuesta, imponer_r7=False)
display(p3_sin_r7)
print(p3_sin_r7.attrs["lectura"])

p3_con_r7 = px.solve_p3(respuesta, imponer_r7=True)
display(p3_con_r7)
print(p3_con_r7.attrs["lectura"])

costo_r7 = p3_sin_r7.attrs["ganancia_aditiva_total"] - p3_con_r7.attrs["ganancia_aditiva_total"]
print(f"\nCosto de imponer R7 (arquitectura de marcas): {costo_r7:.2f} unidades de margen")

# --- celda 17 ---
verificacion_p3 = px.verify_p3_plan(
    bundle, df, supuesto, p3_con_r7.attrs["plan"], p3_con_r7.attrs["ganancia_aditiva_total"]
)
display(verificacion_p3)
print(verificacion_p3.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 18 ---
t0 = time.time()
sensibilidad = px.margin_sensitivity(bundle, df)
display(sensibilidad)
print(sensibilidad.attrs["lectura"])
print(f"[{time.time() - t0:.0f}s]")

# --- celda 19 ---
lift_modelo = metricas_m4["m1"]["lift_top10"]
lift_baseline = metricas_m4["m1"]["lift_top10_politica_historica"]

kpis = px.kpi_table(politicas, p1, p2, verificacion_p3, lift_modelo, lift_baseline, margen_base)
display(kpis)
print(kpis.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 20 ---
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4.5))
plots.plot_value_waterfall(
    margen_base,
    {
        "P1 targeting": politicas[politicas["politica"].str.startswith("P1")]["objetivo_neto"].iloc[0]
        - politicas[politicas["politica"].str.startswith("solo propension")]["objetivo_neto"].iloc[0],
        "P2 calendario": p2.attrs["ganancia"],
        "P3 pricing": verificacion_p3.attrs["ganancia_real"],
    },
    ax=ax,
)
plt.show()

# --- celda 21 ---
impacto = px.business_area_impact(p1, p2, elasticidad_seg, p3_con_r7, base)
display(impacto)
print(impacto.attrs["lectura"])

# --- celda 22 ---
resultados = {
    "supuestos": {
        "margen_bruto": supuesto.margen_bruto,
        "costo_contacto": round(a, 4),
        "costo_promo_dia": round(d, 4),
        "tope_promo_por_marca": supuesto.tope_promo_por_marca,
        "margen_minimo_rho": supuesto.margen_minimo_rho,
        "banda_precio": 0.10,
    },
    "horizonte": {
        "visitas": int(len(base)),
        "dias": [int(base[cfg.KEY_TIME].min()), int(base[cfg.KEY_TIME].max())],
        "margen_base": round(margen_base, 2),
        "ingreso_real": round(float(base["ingreso_real"].sum()), 2),
    },
    "p1": {
        "contactos": p1.attrs["contactos"],
        "clientes": p1.attrs["clientes_distintos"],
        "objetivo": p1.attrs["objetivo"],
        "optimo_verificado": bool(verificacion_p1.attrs["optimo"]),
        "brecha_vs_lp": verificacion_p1.attrs["brecha"],
        "ganancia_vs_politica_historica_pct": politicas.attrs["ganancia_vs_historica_pct"],
        "contactos_utiles": frontera.attrs["contactos_utiles"],
        "pct_visitas_utiles": frontera.attrs["pct_visitas_utiles"],
        "marcas_ofrecidas": p1.attrs["marcas_ofrecidas"],
    },
    "p2": {
        "dias_promo_presupuesto": p2.attrs["dias_gastados_observado"],
        "valor_observado": p2.attrs["valor_observado"],
        "valor_optimo": p2.attrs["valor_optimo"],
        "ganancia": p2.attrs["ganancia"],
        "ganancia_verificada": verificacion_p2.attrs["ganancia_real"],
        "error_aditivo": verificacion_p2.attrs["error_relativo"],
        "dias_por_marca": {int(j): int(v) for j, v in p2["dias_optimos"].items()},
        "valor_por_dia_marca": valores_promo.mean().round(4).to_dict(),
    },
    "p3": {
        "banda": 0.10,
        "plan_con_r7": p3_con_r7.attrs["plan"],
        "plan_sin_r7": p3_sin_r7.attrs["plan"],
        "ganancia_verificada": verificacion_p3.attrs["ganancia_real"],
        "costo_de_r7": round(float(costo_r7), 2),
        "marcas_congeladas": list(px.MARCAS_SIN_ELASTICIDAD),
    },
    "elasticidades_curva": {
        int(j): {"arco_5pct": c.attrs["elasticidad_arco_5pct"],
                 "identificada": c.attrs["identificada"]}
        for j, c in curvas.items()
    },
    "sensibilidad": sensibilidad.to_dict(orient="records"),
    "kpis": kpis.to_dict(orient="records"),
}
payload = px.freeze(resultados)
print("congelado en:", px.PRESCRIPTIVE_PATH.relative_to(ROOT))
print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in payload.items() if k in ("p1", "p2", "p3")}, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

reporte = px.phase5_report(
    curvas, elasticidad_seg, verificacion_p1, politicas, p2, verificacion_p2,
    p3_sin_r7, p3_con_r7, verificacion_p3, kpis, sensibilidad,
)
display(reporte)
